\displaystyle M_{s}\ind\{M_{s}\geq 0\}\log\left(1+\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}+\left|\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right|^{1+\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle-M_{s}\ind\{M_{s}<0\}\log\left(1-\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}+\left|\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right|^{1+\epsilon}\right).

Thus

\displaystyle\exp\left\{M_{s}\Psi_{1}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}+\left|\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right|^{1+\epsilon}\right)^{M_{s}\ind\{M_{s}\geq 0\}}\left(1-\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}+\left|\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right|^{1+\epsilon}\right)^{-M_{s}\ind\{M_{s}<0\}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}1+M_{s}\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}+|M_{s}|\left|\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right|^{1+\epsilon}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}1+M_{s}\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}+\left|\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right|^{1+\epsilon},

where
(a)
holds due to
(1+u)^{v}\leq 1+uv,\forall u\geq-1,v\in(0,1]
and
(b)
holds due to
|M_{s}|\leq 1
.
Then it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left\{M_{s}\Psi_{1}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)\right\}\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[1+M_{s}\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}+\left|\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right|^{1+\epsilon}\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]

\displaystyle=1+\frac{M_{s}}{\tau_{s}}\mathbb{E}[z_{s}^{*}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right|^{1+\epsilon}\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 1+\frac{b^{1+\epsilon}}{\tau_{s}^{1+\epsilon}},

where the first equality holds due to
\frac{M_{s}}{\tau_{s}}\in\mathcal{F}_{s-1}
.
Hence, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left\{\sum_{s=1}^{t}M_{s}\Psi_{1}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}{}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left\{\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}M_{s}\Psi_{1}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)\right\}\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left\{M_{t}\Psi_{1}\left(\frac{z_{t}^{*}}{\tau_{t}}\right)\right\}\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{b^{1+\epsilon}}{\tau_{t}^{1+\epsilon}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left\{\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}M_{s}\Psi_{1}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)\right\}\right]\leq\dots\leq\prod_{s=1}^{t}\left(1+\frac{b^{1+\epsilon}}{\tau_{s}^{1+\epsilon}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\exp\left\{b^{1+\epsilon}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{1}{\tau_{s}^{1+\epsilon}}\right\}=\exp\left\{\left(\frac{b}{\tau_{0}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{w_{s}}{\sqrt{1+w_{s}^{2}}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}s^{-\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\exp\left\{\left(\frac{b}{\tau_{0}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}(2\kappa)^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}\right\}=\exp\left\{\frac{(\sqrt{2\kappa}b)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}}{\tau_{0}^{1+\epsilon}}\right\},

where
(a)
holds due to tower property of conditional expectation and
\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}M_{s}\Psi_{1}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)\in\mathcal{F}_{t-1}
,
(b)
holds due to
1+x\leq\exp\{x\}
and
(c)
holds with the same argument as (
C.11
).

Then, for any
t\geq 1
, a high probability upper bound of
\sum_{s=1}^{t}M_{s}\Psi_{1}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)
can be constructed by

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}M_{s}\Psi_{1}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)\geq\frac{(\sqrt{2\kappa}b)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}}{\tau_{0}^{1+\epsilon}}+\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}{}

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\exp\left\{\sum_{s=1}^{t}M_{s}\Psi_{1}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)\right\}\geq\exp\left\{\frac{(\sqrt{2\kappa}b)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}}{\tau_{0}^{1+\epsilon}}\right\}\cdot\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left\{\sum_{s=1}^{t}M_{s}\Psi_{1}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)\right\}\right]}{\exp\left\{\frac{(\sqrt{2\kappa}b)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}}{\tau_{0}^{1+\epsilon}}\right\}}\frac{\delta}{2t^{2}}\leq\frac{\delta}{2t^{2}},

where
(a)
holds due to the non-decreasing property of
\exp\{x\}
and
(b)
holds due to Markov’s inequality.

Finally, with a union bound over
t\geq 1
and the fact that
\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{2t^{2}}\leq 1
, with probability at least
1-\delta,\forall t\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\Psi_{\tau_{s}}(z_{s}^{*})\frac{2}{1+w_{s}^{2}}\frac{\bm{d}_{s-1}^{\top}\bm{H}_{s-1}^{-1}\bm{\phi}_{s}\ind_{A_{s-1}}}{\sigma_{s}}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(\max_{s\leq t}\alpha_{s})2\tau_{0}t^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}M_{s}\Psi_{1}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle(\max_{s\leq t}\alpha_{s})2\tau_{0}t^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\left[\frac{(\sqrt{2\kappa}b)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}}{\tau_{0}^{1+\epsilon}}+\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}\right]

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(\max_{s\leq t}\alpha_{s})2t^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\left[\frac{(\sqrt{2\kappa}b)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}}{\tau_{0}^{\epsilon}}+\tau_{0}\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}\right].

∎

C.7
Proof of Lemma
C.5

Proof of Lemma
C.5
.

It follows that

\sum_{s=1}^{t}\Psi_{\tau_{s}}^{2}(z_{s}^{*})\frac{w_{s}^{2}}{1+w_{s}^{2}}=\sum_{s=1}^{t}\tau_{s}^{2}\Psi_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)\frac{w_{s}^{2}}{1+w_{s}^{2}}\leq\tau_{0}^{2}t^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{1+\epsilon}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\Psi_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right).

Define
X_{s}:=\Psi_{1}^{2}\left(\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right)
and
Y_{s}=X_{s}-\mathbb{E}[X_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]
, which are
\mathcal{F}_{s}
-measurable. Next we make use of Lemma
I.1
, with probability at least
1-2t^{2}/\delta
, for any
t\geq 1
, we have

\sum_{s=1}^{t}Y_{s}\leq\sqrt{2V\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}}+\frac{2M}{3}\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}

(C.17)

where
\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbb{E}[Y_{s}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]\leq V\text{ and }|Y_{t}|\leq M
.
It follows that
|Y_{s}|\leq|X_{s}|+|\mathbb{E}[X_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]|\leq 2
by
(1)
of Property
1
and

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbb{E}[X_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\frac{z_{s}^{*}}{\tau_{s}}\right|^{1+\epsilon}\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]

(C.18)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{b}{\tau_{s}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}=\left(\frac{b}{\tau_{0}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{w_{s}}{\sqrt{1+w_{s}^{2}}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}s^{-\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\left(\frac{\sqrt{2\kappa}b}{\tau_{0}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}},

where (a) holds due to
(1)
of Property
1
and (b) holds with the same argument as (
C.11
).

Through conducting a similar argument as (
C.18
), we have

\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbb{E}[Y_{s}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]\leq\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbb{E}[X_{s}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]\leq\left(\frac{\sqrt{2\kappa}b}{\tau_{0}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}.

Thus, setting
V=\left(\frac{\sqrt{2\kappa}b}{\tau_{0}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}
and
M=2
in (
C.17
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}X_{s}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbb{E}[X_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]+\sqrt{2V\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}}+\frac{2M}{3}\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\sqrt{2\kappa}b}{\tau_{0}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}+\sqrt{2\left(\frac{\sqrt{2\kappa}b}{\tau_{0}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}}+\frac{4}{3}\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}.

Finally, with a union bound over
t\geq 1
and the fact that
\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{2t^{2}}\leq 1
, with probability at least
1-\delta,\forall t\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\Psi_{\tau_{s}}^{2}(z_{s}^{*})\frac{w_{s}^{2}}{1+w_{s}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\tau_{0}^{2}t^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{1+\epsilon}}\left[\left(\frac{\sqrt{2\kappa}b}{\tau_{0}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}+\sqrt{2\left(\frac{\sqrt{2\kappa}b}{\tau_{0}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}}+\frac{4}{3}\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\tau_{0}^{2}t^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{1+\epsilon}}\left[\sqrt{\left(\frac{\sqrt{2\kappa}b}{\tau_{0}}\right)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}}+\sqrt{2\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\left[t^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\left(\sqrt{\tau_{0}^{1-\epsilon}(\sqrt{2\kappa}b)^{1+\epsilon}(\log 3t)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}}+\tau_{0}\sqrt{2\log\frac{2t^{2}}{\delta}}\right)\right]^{2}.

∎

Appendix D
Proofs for Section
4

D.1
Proof of Theorem
4.1

Proof of Theorem
4.1
.

Note that with probability at least
1-3\delta
, it holds that

\|\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}^{*}\|_{\bm{H}_{t}}\leq\beta_{t}

(D.1)

by Theorem
3.3
with
b=1
. Then we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Regret}(T)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\langle\bm{\phi}_{t}^{*},\bm{\theta}^{*}\rangle-\langle\bm{\phi}_{t},\bm{\theta}^{*}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\max_{\bm{\theta}\in\mathcal{C}_{t-1}}\langle\bm{\phi}_{t}^{*},\bm{\theta}\rangle-\langle\bm{\phi}_{t},\bm{\theta}^{*}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[\max_{\bm{\theta}\in\mathcal{C}_{t-1}}\langle\bm{\phi}_{t},\bm{\theta}\rangle-\langle\bm{\phi}_{t},\bm{\theta}^{*}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{t}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{t-1}^{-1}}\max_{\bm{\theta}\in\mathcal{C}_{t-1}}\left\|\bm{\theta}-\bm{\theta}^{*}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\sum_{t=1}^{T}2\beta_{t}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{t}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{t-1}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}2\beta_{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sigma_{t}w_{t},

where
(a)
holds due to the optimism of action
\bm{\phi}_{t}
,
(b)
holds due to Cauchy-Schwartz inequality,
(c)
holds due to (
D.1
) and
(d)
holds due to the fact that
\beta_{t}
is increasing with
t
.

Notice

\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}^{2}=\min\left\{1,w_{t}^{2}\right\}\leq 2\kappa

(D.2)

by
w_{s}\leq c_{0}\leq 1
and Lemma
I.6
. We will use (
D.2
) several times in the following proof.

Next we bound the sum of bonus
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sigma_{t}w_{t}
separately by the value of
\sigma_{t}
. Recall the definition of
\sigma_{t}
in Algorithm
2
, we decompose
[T]
as the union of three disjoint sets
\mathcal{J}_{1},\mathcal{J}_{2},\mathcal{J}_{3}
where

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{1}

\displaystyle=\left\{t\in[T]|\sigma_{t}\in\left\{\nu_{t},\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}\right\}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\{t\in[T]\bigg|\sigma_{t}=\frac{\|\bm{\phi}_{t}\|_{\bm{H}_{t-1}^{-1}}}{c_{0}}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{3}

\displaystyle=\left\{t\in[T]\Bigg|\sigma_{t}=\frac{\sqrt{LB}}{c_{1}^{\frac{1}{4}}(2\kappa)^{\frac{1}{4}}}\|\bm{\phi}_{t}\|_{\bm{H}_{t-1}^{-1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}.

For the summation over
\mathcal{J}_{1}
, we have

\sum_{t\in\mathcal{J}_{1}}\sigma_{t}w_{t}=\sum_{t\in\mathcal{J}_{1}}\max\left\{\nu_{t},\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}\right\}w_{t}\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\nu_{t}^{2}+\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}^{2})}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}^{2}}\overset{(b)}{\leq}\sqrt{2\kappa}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\nu_{t}^{2}+\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}^{2})},

where
(a)
holds due to Cauchy-Schwartz inequality and
(b)
holds due to (
D.2
).

And for the summation over
\mathcal{J}_{2}
, we have
w_{t}=\left\|\frac{\bm{\phi}_{t}}{\sigma_{t}}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{t-1}^{-1}}=c_{0}
. Then

\sum_{t\in\mathcal{J}_{2}}\sigma_{t}w_{t}=\frac{1}{c_{0}^{2}}\sum_{t\in\mathcal{J}_{2}}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{t}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{t-1}^{-1}}w_{t}^{2}\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{L}{c_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\lambda}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}w_{t}^{2}\overset{(b)}{\leq}\frac{2L\kappa}{c_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\lambda}},

where
(a)
holds due to
\bm{H}_{t-1}\succeq\lambda\bm{I}
, thus
\left\|\bm{\phi}_{t}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{t-1}^{-1}}\leq\frac{\left\|\bm{\phi}_{t}\right\|}{\sqrt{\lambda}}\leq\frac{L}{\sqrt{\lambda}}
. And
(b)
holds due to (
D.2
).

Then, for the summation over
\mathcal{J}_{3}
, we have
\sigma_{t}=\frac{LB}{\sqrt{2c_{1}\kappa}}w_{t}
. Therefore

\sum_{t\in\mathcal{J}_{3}}\sigma_{t}w_{t}=\frac{LB}{\sqrt{2c_{1}\kappa}}\sum_{t\in\mathcal{J}_{3}}w_{t}^{2}\leq\frac{LB\sqrt{2\kappa}}{\sqrt{c_{1}}},

where the inequality holds due to (
D.2
).

Finally, putting pieces together finishes the proof.
∎

Appendix E
Proof of Theorem
5.1

Proof of Theorem
5.1
.

Recall our proposed algorithm
Heavy-LSVI-UCB
is detailed in Algorithm
3
.
First, to compute
\bm{\theta}_{k-1,h}
in line
6
, we notice the loss function in (
5.1
) is
\lambda_{R}
-strongly convex and
(\lambda_{R}+K/\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2})
-smooth, so there are plenty of convex optimization algorithms available. For example, Nesterov accelerated method can be used. According to
Bubeck et al. 2015
, the number of iteration of Nesterov’s method is
{O}(\sqrt{\beta/\alpha}\log(R^{2}/\epsilon))
with one derivation (
O(d)
operations) per iteration. Here the loss function is supposed to be
\alpha
-strongly convex and
\beta
-smooth.
R
is the maximum distance of two points and
\epsilon
is the precision. Thus the total computational cost is
\widetilde{O}(HK\mathcal{R})
with
\mathcal{R}=\widetilde{O}\left(d\sqrt{1+K/(\lambda_{R}\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}})\right)=\widetilde{O}\left(d+d^{-\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}H^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}K^{\frac{1+2\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\right)
.

Second, to evaluate the updated action-value function
Q^{k}_{h}(s,a)
in line
10
for a given pair
(s,a)
, we take the minimum over at most
\widetilde{O}(dH)
action-value functions (See Lemma
I.8
) with
O(d^{2})
operations (Using Sherman-Morrison formula to compute
H_{k-1,h}^{-1}
and
\Sigma_{k-1,h}^{-1}
) for each function. Thus it takes
\widetilde{O}(d^{3}H)
to evaluate the updated action-value function. As a result, to compute
\widehat{w}_{k-1,h}
in line
6
, notice
\widehat{w}_{k,h}=\Sigma_{k,h}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\phi_{i,h}V^{k}_{h}(s_{i,h+1})
, if
V^{k}_{h}
remains unchanged, we only need to compute the new term
\sigma_{k,h}^{-2}\phi_{k,h}V^{k}_{h}(s_{k,h+1})
, which takes
\widetilde{O}(d^{3}|\mathcal{A}|H)
computational time. Else if
V^{k}_{h}
is updated, we need to recalculate
\{V^{k}_{h}(s_{i,h+1})\}_{i\in[k]}
, which takes
\widetilde{O}(d^{3}|\mathcal{A}|HK)
computational time. Note the number of updating episode is at most
\widetilde{O}(dH)
and the length of each episode is
H
, so the total computational cost is
\widetilde{O}(d^{4}|\mathcal{A}|H^{3}K)
.

Last, to take action
a_{k,h}
in line
19
, we need to compute
\{Q^{k}_{h}(s_{k,h},a)\}_{a\in\mathcal{A}}
and take the maximum, which takes
\widetilde{O}(d^{3}|\mathcal{A}|H)
time, incurring a total cost of
\widetilde{O}(d^{3}|\mathcal{A}|H^{2}K)
. Finally, combining the total costs above gives the computational complexity of
Heavy-LSVI-UCB
.
∎

Appendix F
Proof of Theorem
5.2

In this section, we give the proof sketch of Theorem
5.2
. In Appendix
F.1
, leveraging the technique in
He et al. 2023
, we define several events and prove them hold with high probability.
In Appendix
F.2
, we then decompose the regret into a lower order term and summations of bonus terms with respect to reward functions and transition probabilities. Finally, we adopt a novel approach that deal with the two bonus terms separately.

F.1
High-Probability Events

Parameters for Adaptive Huber Regression in Algorithm
3
.

First, we set the parameters for adaptive Huber regression in Algorithm
3
as follows:

\displaystyle c_{0}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{23\log\frac{2HK^{2}}{\delta}}},\quad c_{1}=\min\left\{\frac{(\log 3K)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{1+\epsilon}}}{48\left(\log\frac{2HK^{2}}{\delta}\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}},\frac{(\log 3K)^{\frac{1-\epsilon^{\prime}}{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}}{48\left(\log\frac{2HK^{2}}{\delta}\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}}\right\},

\displaystyle\tau_{0}=\frac{\sqrt{2\kappa}(\log 3K)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}}{\left(\log\frac{2HK^{2}}{\delta}\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}}},\quad\widetilde{\tau}_{0}=\frac{\sqrt{2\kappa}\nu_{R^{\epsilon}}(\log 3K)^{\frac{1-\epsilon^{\prime}}{2(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}}}{\nu_{\mathrm{min}}\left(\log\frac{2HK^{2}}{\delta}\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}}.

Measurability.

We define filtration
\left\{\mathcal{G}_{k,h}\right\}_{(k,h)\in[K]\times[H]}
and
\left\{\mathcal{F}_{k,h}\right\}_{(k,h)\in[K]\times[H]}
as follows. Let
I_{k,h}:=\{(i,j):i\in[k-1],j\in[H]\ \text{or}\ i=k,j\in[h]\}
denote the set of index pairs up to and including the
k
-th episode and the
h
-th step. We further define
\mathcal{G}_{k,h}=\sigma\left(\bigcup_{(i,j)\in I_{k,h-1}}\left\{s_{i,j},a_{i,j},r_{i,j}\right\}\cup\left\{s_{k,h},a_{k,h}\right\}\right)
and
\mathcal{F}_{k,h}=\sigma\left(\bigcup_{(i,j)\in I_{k,h}}\left\{s_{i,j},a_{i,j},r_{i,j}\right\}\right)
.
We make a convention that
\mathcal{G}_{k,0}=\mathcal{G}_{k-1,H+1}
and
\mathcal{F}_{k,0}=\mathcal{F}_{k-1,H}
.
Note
\mathcal{G}_{k,h}\subset\mathcal{F}_{k,h}\subset\mathcal{G}_{k,h+1}
.

We first introduce the following high-probability events:

1.

We define
\mathcal{E}_{R^{\epsilon}}
as the event that the following inequalities hold for all
k,h\in[K]\times[H]
,

\left\|\bm{\psi}_{k,h}-\bm{\psi}_{h}^{*}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k,h}^{-1}}\leq\beta_{R^{\epsilon},k},

where
\bm{\psi}_{k,h}
is defined in (
5.2
)

\beta_{R^{\epsilon},k}=3\sqrt{\lambda_{R}}W+24k^{\frac{1-\epsilon^{\prime}}{2(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}}\frac{\sqrt{2\kappa}\nu_{R^{\epsilon}}}{\nu_{\mathrm{min}}}(\log 3K)^{\frac{1-\epsilon^{\prime}}{2(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}}\left(\log\frac{2HK^{2}}{\delta}\right)^{\frac{\epsilon^{\prime}}{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}}.

(F.1)

For simplicity, we further define
\beta_{R^{\epsilon}}:=\beta_{R^{\epsilon},K}
.

2.

We define
\mathcal{E}_{0}
as the event that the following inequalities hold for all
k,h\in[K]\times[H]
,

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{0},

\displaystyle\left\|\check{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[\check{V}^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{0},

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[\left(V^{k}_{h+1}\right)^{2}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{H}\beta_{0},

where
\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h},\check{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h},\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}
are defined in (
5.8
) and

\beta_{0}=2\sqrt{d\lambda_{V}}\mathcal{H}+\frac{3\mathcal{H}}{\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}}\sqrt{d^{3}H\iota_{0}^{2}+\log\frac{H}{\delta}},

(F.2)

and

\displaystyle\iota_{0}=\max\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\log_{2}\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda_{R}\nu_{\min}^{2}}\right),\log_{2}\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda_{V}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right),\log\left(1+\frac{8(B+L)K}{\lambda_{V}\mathcal{H}\sqrt{d}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right),

\displaystyle\log\left(1+\frac{32\beta_{R}^{2}K^{2}}{\sqrt{d}\lambda_{R}\lambda_{V}^{2}\mathcal{H}^{2}\sigma_{\min}^{4}}\right),\log\left(1+\frac{32\beta_{V}^{2}K^{2}}{\sqrt{d}\lambda_{V}^{3}\mathcal{H}^{2}\sigma_{\min}^{4}}\right)\Bigg\},L=\mathcal{H}\sqrt{\frac{dK}{\lambda_{V}}}.

3.

We define
\mathcal{E}_{R,k}
as the event that the following inequalities hold for all
i,h\in[k]\times[H]
,

\left\|\bm{\theta}_{i,h}-\bm{\theta}_{h}^{*}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{i,h}^{-1}}\leq\beta_{R,i},

where
\bm{\theta}_{i,h}
is defined in (
5.1
) and

\beta_{R,i}=O\left(\sqrt{\lambda_{R}}B+\sqrt{d}i^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\iota\right),

(F.3)

and

\iota=\max\left\{\log\left(1+\frac{K}{d\lambda_{R}\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}}\right),\log 3K,\log\frac{2HK^{2}}{\delta}\right\}.

For simplicity, we further define
\beta_{R}:=\beta_{R,K}=O\left(\sqrt{\lambda_{R}}B+\sqrt{d}K^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\iota\right)
and
\mathcal{E}_{R}:=\mathcal{E}_{R,K}
.

4.

We define
\mathcal{E}_{h}
as the event that the following inequalities hold for all
k\in[K],h\leq h^{\prime}\leq H
,

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h^{\prime}}-\bm{w}_{h^{\prime}}\left[V^{k}_{h^{\prime}+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{V},

\displaystyle\left\|\check{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h^{\prime}}-\bm{w}_{h^{\prime}}\left[\check{V}^{k}_{h^{\prime}+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{V}.

where

\beta_{V}=O\left(\sqrt{d\lambda_{V}}\mathcal{H}+\sqrt{d}\iota_{1}^{2}\right),

(F.4)

and

\displaystyle\iota_{1}=\max\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\iota_{0},\log\left(1+\frac{K}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}d\lambda_{V}}\right),\log\frac{4HK^{2}}{\delta},\log\left(1+\frac{4(B+L)\sqrt{d^{3}H}}{\sigma_{\min}}\right),

\displaystyle\log\left(1+\frac{8\sqrt{d^{7}}H\beta_{R}^{2}}{\lambda_{R}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right),\log\left(1+\frac{8\sqrt{d^{7}}H\beta_{V}^{2}}{\lambda_{V}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)\Bigg\},L=\mathcal{H}\sqrt{\frac{dK}{\lambda_{V}}}.

For simplicity, we further define
\mathcal{E}_{V}:=\mathcal{E}_{1}
.

Our ultimate goal is to show
\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{V}
holds with high probability, which is a ‘refined’ event where the radius
\beta_{R},\beta_{V}
are smaller than
\beta_{R^{\epsilon}},\beta_{0}
in the ‘coarse’ event
\mathcal{E}_{R^{\epsilon}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{0}
. Leveraging the technique in
He et al. 2023
, we first prove event
\mathcal{E}_{R^{\epsilon}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{0}
holds with high probability, then come to
\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{V}
.

Lemma F.1
.

Event
\mathcal{E}_{R^{\epsilon}}
holds with probability at least
1-3\delta
.

Proof.

See Appendix
H.1
for a detailed proof.
∎

and

) and (

) respectively.

F.2
Regret Analysis

In this section, we will prove the regret bound based on the events defined in Appendix
F.1
, which hold with probability at least
1-11\delta
. By the optimism of Lemma
F.5
, we have

\mathrm{Regret}(K)=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[V^{*}_{1}(s_{k,1})-V^{\pi^{k}}_{1}(s_{k,1})\right]\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[V^{k}_{1}(s_{k,1})-V^{\pi^{k}}_{1}(s_{k,1})\right].

Next we bound the regret with the summations of two bonus terms, i.e.
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}
and
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}
with respect to reward functions and transition probabilities by Lemma
F.9
. Then we bound them separately by Lemma
F.11
and Lemma
F.12
.

Lemma F.9
.

With probability at least
1-\delta
, on event
\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{V}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[V^{k}_{1}(s_{k,1})-V^{\pi^{k}}_{1}(s_{k,1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 6\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+6\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+36H\mathcal{H}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}.

and

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 8H\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+8H\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+29H^{2}\mathcal{H}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}.

Proof.

See Appendix
H.9
for a detailed proof.
∎

Lemma F.10
.

With probability at least
1-\delta
, on event
\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{V}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{k}-\check{V}_{h+1}^{k})\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 8H\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+16H\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+29H^{2}\mathcal{H}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}.

Proof.

See Appendix
H.10
for a detailed proof.
∎

Lemma F.11
.

Set
\lambda_{R}=\frac{d}{\max\left\{B^{2},W^{2}\right\}}
. Then with probability at least
1-\delta
, on event
\mathcal{E}_{R^{\epsilon}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{R}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=\widetilde{O}\Bigg(

\displaystyle\sqrt{dH\mathcal{U}^{*}K}+\sqrt{dH^{2}K\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}}+\Bigg(\frac{\nu_{R^{\epsilon}}d^{\frac{2+\epsilon}{2}}H^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}K^{\frac{1-\epsilon^{\prime}}{2(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}}}{\nu_{\mathrm{min}}}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}

\displaystyle+\left(d^{\frac{4+\epsilon}{2}}H^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}K^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}+(\nu_{R}+\max\left\{B,W\right\})\sqrt{d}H\Bigg).

Choosing
\nu_{\mathrm{min}}=\widetilde{O}\left(\nu_{R^{\epsilon}}^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}}d^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}}H^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}K^{-\frac{(1+\epsilon)(1+\epsilon^{\prime})-2}{2(1+\epsilon)(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}}\right)
further gives

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=\widetilde{O}\bigg(

\displaystyle\sqrt{dH\mathcal{U}^{*}K}+\nu_{R^{\epsilon}}^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}}d^{\frac{3+\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}H^{\frac{3+\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}K^{\frac{1}{(1+\epsilon)(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}}

\displaystyle+\left(d^{\frac{4+\epsilon}{2}}H^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}K^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}+(\nu_{R}+\max\left\{B,W\right\})\sqrt{d}H\bigg).

Proof.

See Appendix
H.11
for a detailed proof.
∎

Lemma F.12
.

Set
\lambda_{V}=\frac{1}{\mathcal{H}^{2}}
, let
\mathcal{A}_{0}
denote the event where Lemma
F.9
and Lemma
F.10
hold. Then with probability at least
1-2\delta
, on event
\mathcal{E}_{0}\cap\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{V}\cap\mathcal{A}_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=\widetilde{O}\Bigg(

\displaystyle\sqrt{dH\mathcal{V}^{*}K}+\sqrt{dH^{2}K\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}}+\frac{d^{5.5}H^{2.5}\mathcal{H}^{2}}{\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{d^{4}H^{3}\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}}+d^{4.5}H^{3}\mathcal{H}\bigg).

Choosing
\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}=\widetilde{O}\left(\sqrt{d^{5}H^{1.5}\mathcal{H}^{2}}K^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)
further gives

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=\widetilde{O}\Bigg(

\displaystyle\sqrt{dH\mathcal{V}^{*}K}+\sqrt{d^{6}H^{3.5}\mathcal{H}^{2}}K^{\frac{1}{4}}+\sqrt{d^{4}H^{3}\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}}

\displaystyle+d^{4.5}H^{3}\mathcal{H}\Bigg).

Proof.

See Appendix
H.12
for a detailed proof.
∎

At the end of this section, we state the formal version of Theorem
5.2
and provide its proof.

Theorem F.13

(Formal version of Theorem
5.2
)
.

For the time-inhomogeneous linear MDPs with heavy-tailed rewards defined in Section
2.2
, we set parameters in Algorithm
3
as follows:
\lambda_{R}=d/\max\left\{B^{2},W^{2}\right\}
,
\lambda_{V}=1/\mathcal{H}^{2}
,

\nu_{\mathrm{min}}
in Lemma
F.11
,
\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}
in Lemma
F.12
,

\beta_{R^{\epsilon}}
,
\beta_{0}
,
\beta_{R}
,
\beta_{V}
in (
F.1
), (
F.2
), (
F.3
), (
F.4
), respectively.
Then for any
\delta\in(0,1)
, with probability at least
1-16\delta
, the regret of
Heavy-LSVI-UCB
is bounded by

\displaystyle\mathrm{Regret}(K)=\widetilde{O}\bigg(

\displaystyle d\sqrt{H\mathcal{U}^{*}}K^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}}+d\sqrt{H\mathcal{V}^{*}K}+\nu_{R^{\epsilon}}^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}}d^{\frac{2+\epsilon}{1+\epsilon}}H^{\frac{3+\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}K^{\frac{2+(1-\epsilon)(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}{2(1+\epsilon)(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{d^{7}H^{3.5}\mathcal{H}^{2}}K^{\frac{1}{4}}+\left(d^{2+\epsilon}H^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}K^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}

\displaystyle+(\nu_{R}+\max\left\{B,W\right\})dHK^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}+d^{5}H^{3}\mathcal{H}\bigg),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}^{*}

\displaystyle=\min\left\{\mathcal{U}^{*}_{0},\mathcal{U}\right\}\text{ with }\mathcal{U}^{*}_{0}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{(s_{h},a_{h})\sim d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right]^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}(s_{h},a_{h})\right],

(F.5)

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}^{*}

\displaystyle=\min\left\{\mathcal{V}^{*}_{0},\mathcal{V}\right\}\text{ with }\mathcal{V}^{*}_{0}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{(s_{h},a_{h})\sim d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{h},a_{h})\right],

(F.6)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\,\,d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(s,a)=\mathbb{P}^{\pi^{k}}((s_{h},a_{h})=(s,a)|s_{0}=s_{k,1})

(F.7)

and
\mathcal{H},\mathcal{U},\mathcal{V}
are defined in Assumption
2.7
.

Proof of Theorem
5.2
.

Based on high-probability events in Appendix
F.1
and Lemmas in this section, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Regret}(K)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[V^{*}_{1}(s_{k,1})-V^{\pi^{k}}_{1}(s_{k,1})\right]\overset{(a)}{\leq}{}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[V^{k}_{1}(s_{k,1})-V^{\pi^{k}}_{1}(s_{k,1})\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 6\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+6\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+36H\mathcal{H}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}{}

\displaystyle\widetilde{O}\bigg(d\sqrt{H\mathcal{U}^{*}}K^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}}+d\sqrt{H\mathcal{V}^{*}K}+\nu_{R^{\epsilon}}^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}}d^{\frac{2+\epsilon}{1+\epsilon}}H^{\frac{3+\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}K^{\frac{2+(1-\epsilon)(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}{2(1+\epsilon)(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}}+\sqrt{d^{7}H^{3.5}\mathcal{H}^{2}}K^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle+\left(d^{2+\epsilon}H^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}K^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}+(\nu_{R}+\max\left\{B,W\right\})dHK^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}+d^{5}H^{3}\mathcal{H}\bigg),

where
(a)
holds due to the optimism of Lemma
F.5
, (b) holds due to Lemma
F.9
. (c) holds due to Lemma
F.11
, Lemma
F.12
and

\beta_{V}\sqrt{d^{4}H^{3}\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\beta_{V}^{2}d^{4}H^{3}\mathcal{H}+\frac{1}{2}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}.

∎

Remark F.14
.

If
\epsilon>\frac{1}{1+\epsilon^{\prime}}
, then
K^{\frac{2+(1-\epsilon)(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}{2(1+\epsilon)(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}}<\sqrt{K}
. When the number of episodes
K
is sufficiently large, the regret can be simplified to
\widetilde{O}\left(d\sqrt{H\mathcal{U}^{*}}K^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}}+d\sqrt{H\mathcal{V}^{*}K}\right)
.

Remark F.15
.

When
\epsilon=1
, we return to the setting in
Li and Sun 2023
and the regret reduces to
\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{H\mathcal{U}^{*}K}+d\sqrt{H\mathcal{V}^{*}K})
. While their variance-aware regret bound is
\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{H\mathcal{G}^{*}K})
, where
\mathcal{G}^{*}
is a variance-dependent quantity defined in their work. We provide the definition of
\mathcal{G}^{*}
and its relationship with
\mathcal{U}^{*}
,
\mathcal{V}^{*}
below.

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{*}

\displaystyle=\min\left\{\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{(s_{h},a_{h})\sim d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}R_{h}+\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{h},a_{h})\right],\mathcal{U}+\mathcal{V}\right\}

\displaystyle=\min\left\{\mathcal{U}^{*}_{0}+\mathcal{V}^{*}_{0},\mathcal{U}+\mathcal{V}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq\min\left\{\mathcal{U}^{*}_{0},\mathcal{U}\right\}+\min\left\{\mathcal{V}^{*}_{0},\mathcal{V}\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{U}^{*}+\mathcal{V}^{*},

where
\mathcal{U}^{*}
,
\mathcal{V}^{*}
and
d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(s,a)
are defined in Theorem
5.2
.
Thus we have
d\sqrt{H\mathcal{U}^{*}K}+d\sqrt{H\mathcal{V}^{*}K}\leq 2d\sqrt{H(\mathcal{U}^{*}+\mathcal{V}^{*})K}\leq 2d\sqrt{H\mathcal{G}^{*}K}
, which implies that we recover their result.

F.3
Proof of Corollary
5.6

Proof of Corollary
5.6
.

First, it holds that
\mathcal{V}^{*}\leq\mathcal{V}\leq\mathcal{H}V^{*}_{1}
according to (
H.19
) in the proof of Lemma
H.4
in Appendix
H.16
.
Thus the first result follows.
Next, to make a fair comparison with the state-of-the-art result of first-order regret
[
33
]
, we assume the reward functions are uniformly bounded, i.e.,
R_{h}(s,a)\in[0,1]
for all
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
and
h\in[H]
.
Then
\epsilon=1
,
\mathcal{H}=H
and

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}^{*}\leq\mathcal{U}^{*}_{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{(s_{h},a_{h})\sim d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right]^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}(s_{h},a_{h})\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{(s_{h},a_{h})\sim d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}r_{h}(s_{h},a_{h})\right]\overset{(b)}{\leq}V^{*}_{1},

where
(a)
holds due to
R_{h}(s,a)
is bounded in
[0,1]
for all
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
and
h\in[H]
,
(b)
uses the optimality of
V^{*}_{1}
.
Finally, the proof is completed by the fact that
\mathcal{V}^{*}\leq HV^{*}_{1}
and
\mathcal{U}^{*}\leq V^{*}_{1}
.
∎

Appendix G
Proof of Lower Bound

Proof of Theorem
5.5
.

The proof of Theorem
5.5
follows from a combination of the lower bound constructions for heavy-tailed linear bandits in
Shao et al. 2018
and linear MDPs in
Zhou et al. 2021
.
On one hand, we construct a linear MDP with deterministic transition probabilities by concatenating
H
hard instances in
Shao et al. 2018
together. Summing the regret over the
H
components yields
\Omega(dHK^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}})
.
On the other hand,
Zhou et al. 2021
shows the regret is at least
\Omega(d\sqrt{H^{3}K})
. Combining the results together gives the final lower bound.
∎

Appendix H
Omitted Proofs in Appendix
F

H.1
Proof of Lemma
F.1

Proof of Lemma
F.1
.

For each
h\in[H]
, we apply Theorem
3.3
with
L=1,B=W,T=k,\delta=\delta/H,\mathcal{F}_{t}=\mathcal{G}_{i,h},y_{t}=|\varepsilon_{i,h}|^{1+\epsilon},\bm{\theta}_{t}=\bm{\psi}_{k,h}
and
b=\nu_{R^{\epsilon}}/\nu_{\mathrm{min}}
. We choose parameters
c_{0},c_{1},\widetilde{\tau}_{0}
as in Theorem
3.3
. Then with probability at least
1-3\delta
,
\left\|\bm{\psi}_{k,h}-\bm{\psi}_{h}^{*}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k,h}^{-1}}\leq\beta_{R^{\epsilon},k}
, that is event
\mathcal{E}_{R^{\epsilon}}
holds.
∎

H.2
Proof of Lemma
F.2

Proof of Lemma
F.2
.

Notice that

\displaystyle\left|\left[\widehat{\bm{\nu}}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}-\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\left|\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widehat{\bm{\psi}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{\psi}^{*}_{h}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left|\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widehat{\bm{\psi}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{\psi}_{k-1,h}\rangle\right|+\left|\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\bm{\psi}_{k-1,h}-\bm{\psi}^{*}_{h}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\|\widehat{\bm{\psi}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{\psi}_{k-1,h}\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}}+\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\left\|\bm{\psi}_{k-1,h}-\bm{\psi}^{*}_{h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}},

where the last inequality holds due to Cauchy-Schwartz inequality. And

\left\|\bm{\psi}_{k-1,h}-\bm{\psi}^{*}_{h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}}\leq\beta_{R^{\epsilon},k-1}

(H.1)

since
\mathcal{E}_{R^{\epsilon}}
holds.

We next give an upper bound of
\|\widehat{\bm{\psi}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{\psi}_{k-1,h}\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}}
by a novel perturbation analysis of adaptive Huber regression. For each
h\in[H]
, we apply Lemma
C.3
with
t=k-1,\bm{\theta}_{t}=\bm{\psi}_{k-1,h},\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{t}=\widehat{\bm{\psi}}_{k-1,h},\mathcal{F}_{s}=\mathcal{G}_{i,h},y_{s}=|\varepsilon_{i,h}|^{1+\epsilon},\widehat{y}_{s}=|\widehat{\varepsilon}_{i,h}|^{1+\epsilon}
. Notice

\displaystyle\left||\widehat{\varepsilon}_{i,h}|^{1+\epsilon}-|\varepsilon_{i,h}|^{1+\epsilon}\right|

\displaystyle\leq|\widehat{\varepsilon}_{i,h}-\varepsilon_{i,h}|^{1+\epsilon}

\displaystyle=\left|\langle\bm{\phi}_{i,h},\bm{\theta}_{i,h}-\bm{\theta}^{*}_{h}\rangle\right|^{1+\epsilon}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}\left\|\bm{\theta}_{i,h}-\bm{\theta}^{*}_{h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{i,h}}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{i,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}\beta_{R,i-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{i,h}^{-1}},

where
(a)
holds due to Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, and
\langle\bm{\phi}_{i,h},\bm{\theta}_{i,h}\rangle
together with
\langle\bm{\phi}_{i,h},\bm{\theta}^{*}_{h}\rangle
are bounded in
[0,\mathcal{H}]
. And
(b)
is due to
\mathcal{E}_{R,k-1}
holds.
Then we have
\widehat{\beta}_{s}=\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}\beta_{R,i-1}
in Lemma
C.3
, and it follows that

\|\widehat{\bm{\psi}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{\psi}_{k-1,h}\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}}\leq 6\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}\beta_{R,k-1}\kappa.

(H.2)

Combining (
H.1
) with (
H.2
), we have

\left|\left[\widehat{\bm{\nu}}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}-\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|\leq(\beta_{R^{\epsilon},k-1}+6\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}\beta_{R,k-1}\kappa)\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}.

That is
\left|\left[\widehat{\bm{\nu}}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}-\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|\leq W_{k,h}
.
∎

H.3
Proof of Lemma
F.3

Proof of Lemma
F.3
.

For each
h\in[H]
, we use Theorem
3.3
with
L=1,\delta=\delta/H,T=k,\mathcal{F}_{t}=\mathcal{G}_{i,h},y_{t}=\widetilde{R}_{i,h}:=R_{i,h}\ind\left\{\left|\left[\widehat{\bm{\nu}}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}-\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right|\leq W_{i,h}\right\}
. Thus
b=1
by definition. Denote
\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}_{k,h}
as the counterpart of
\bm{\theta}_{k,h}
being the solution of adaptive Huber regression where
R_{i,h}
is replaced by
\widetilde{R}_{i,h}
for all
i\leq k
. Then by Theorem
3.3
, with probability at least
1-3\delta
, we have

\left\|\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}_{k,h}-\bm{\theta}_{h}^{*}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k,h}^{-1}}\leq\beta_{R,k}

(H.3)

for all
h\in[H]
and
k\in[K]
.

Then, we continue the proof by induction on event
\mathcal{E}_{R,k}
over
k
. First, event
\mathcal{E}_{R,0}
holds trivially. Next, we suppose
\mathcal{E}_{R,k-1}
holds and will prove
\mathcal{E}_{R,k}
holds. Since
\mathcal{E}_{R^{\epsilon}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{R,k-1}
holds, it follows that
\widetilde{R}_{i,h}=R_{i,h}
for all
h\in[H]
and
i\leq k
by Lemma
F.2
and the definition of
\widetilde{R}_{i,h}
. And thus
\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}_{k,h}=\bm{\theta}_{k,h}
. By (
H.3
), for all
h\in[H]
, we have

\left\|\bm{\theta}_{i,h}-\bm{\theta}_{h}^{*}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{i,h}^{-1}}\leq\beta_{R,i},

where

\displaystyle\beta_{R,i}

\displaystyle=3\sqrt{\lambda_{R}}B+24i^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\sqrt{2\kappa}(\log 3K)^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\left(\log\frac{2HK^{2}}{\delta}\right)^{\frac{\epsilon}{1+\epsilon}}

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{\lambda_{R}}B+\sqrt{d}i^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\iota\right).

That is
\mathcal{E}_{R,k}
holds.

Finally, induction over all
k\in[K]
completes the proof.
∎

H.4
Proof of Lemma
F.4

We will use the self-normalized bound with a covering argument in Lemma
I.5
for estimating next-state value function in the following proof frequently, which is the core technique used in
Jin et al. 2020
,
He et al. 2023
.

Proof of Lemma
F.4
.

Define
\mathcal{V}^{+}
as the class of optimistic value functions mapping from
\mathcal{S}
to
\mathbb{R}
with the parametric form given in (
I.2
) and
\mathcal{V}^{-}
the class of pessimistic value functions with the parametric form given in (
I.3
).
By Lemma
I.8
and Lemma
I.9
,

\log\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V}^{\pm},\epsilon)\leq\left[d\log\left(1+\frac{4(B+L)}{\epsilon}\right)+d^{2}\log\left(1+\frac{8d^{1/2}\beta_{R,K}^{2}}{\lambda_{R}\epsilon^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{8d^{1/2}\beta_{V}^{2}}{\lambda_{V}\epsilon^{2}}\right)\right]|\mathcal{K}|,

(H.4)

where
L=\mathcal{H}\sqrt{\frac{dK}{\lambda_{V}}}
and
|\mathcal{K}|\leq dH\log_{2}\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda_{R}\nu_{\min}^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda_{V}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)
.

The case where
f=V_{h+1}^{k}
.

One can find that
f\in\mathcal{V}^{+}
.
To make use of Lemma
I.5
, we first specify the parameters defined therein.
We have
\|f\|_{\infty}\leq C_{0}=\mathcal{H}
and
\epsilon_{1}=\frac{\lambda_{V}\mathcal{H}\sqrt{d}}{K}\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}
.
By (
H.4
), it follows that

\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V}^{+},\epsilon_{1})

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left[d\log\left(1+\frac{4(B+L)K}{\lambda_{V}\mathcal{H}\sqrt{d}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)+d^{2}\log\left(1+\frac{8\beta_{R,K}^{2}K^{2}}{\sqrt{d}\lambda_{R}\lambda_{V}^{2}\mathcal{H}^{2}\sigma_{\min}^{4}}\right)\left(1+\frac{8\beta_{V}^{2}K^{2}}{\sqrt{d}\lambda_{V}^{3}\mathcal{H}^{2}\sigma_{\min}^{4}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot dH\log_{2}\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda_{R}\nu_{\min}^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda_{V}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 6d^{3}H\iota_{0}^{2}.

By the third condition of Lemma
I.5
, with probability at least
1-\frac{\delta}{H}
,
\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}}\leq\beta_{0}
for all
k\in[K]
.
A union which finishes the proof.

The case where
f=\check{V}_{h+1}^{k}
.

The analysis on
\check{V}_{h+1}^{k}
is similar to (i).

The case where
f=\left(V_{h+1}^{k}\right)^{2}
.

The analysis on
\left(V_{h+1}^{k}\right)^{2}
is similar to (i) except for the following two changes.
First,
C_{0}=\mathcal{H}^{2}
and
\epsilon_{1}^{\prime}=\frac{\lambda_{V}\mathcal{H}^{2}\sqrt{d}}{K}\sigma_{\min}^{2}
.
Second, with
[\mathcal{V}^{+}]^{2}=\{f^{2}:f\in\mathcal{V}^{+}\}
, we have
[V_{h+1}^{k}]^{2}\in[\mathcal{V}^{+}]^{2}
and

\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}([\mathcal{V}^{+}]^{2},\epsilon_{1}^{\prime})

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\log\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V}^{+},\frac{\epsilon_{1}^{\prime}}{2\mathcal{H}})\leq\log\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V}^{+},\frac{\epsilon_{1}}{2})\leq 6d^{3}H\iota_{0}^{2}.

Here
(a)
uses the fact that the
\frac{\epsilon_{1}^{\prime}}{2\mathcal{H}}
-cover of
\mathcal{V}^{+}
is a
\epsilon_{1}
-cover of
[\mathcal{V}^{+}]^{2}
.
∎

H.5
Proof of Lemma
F.5

Proof of Lemma
F.5
.

We prove the optimism inequality by induction. When
h=H+1
, results hold trivially since
V^{k}_{H+1}=V^{*}_{H+1}=0
. We assume the statement is true for
h+1
, that is
V^{k}_{h+1}(\cdot)\geq V^{*}_{h+1}(\cdot)
. Next we prove the case of
h
.

For any
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
and
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot),\bm{\theta}_{k-1,h}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\rangle+\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}-Q^{*}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot),\bm{\theta}_{k-1,h}-\bm{\theta}^{*}_{h}\rangle+\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot),\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{*}_{h+1}\right]\rangle+\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\geq}{}

\displaystyle\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot),\bm{\theta}_{k-1,h}-\bm{\theta}^{*}_{h}\rangle+\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot),\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]\rangle+\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\geq}{}

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\left(-\left\|\bm{\theta}_{k-1,h}-\bm{\theta}^{*}_{h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}}+\beta_{R,k-1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\left(-\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}+\beta_{V}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\geq}{}

\displaystyle 0,

where
(a)
is due to
\mathcal{E}_{h}
holds,
\mathcal{E}_{h+1}\subset\mathcal{E}_{h}
and thus
\left\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot),\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{*}_{h+1}\right]\right\rangle=[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1})](\cdot,\cdot)\geq 0
by induction.
(b)
is due to Cauchy-Schwartz inequality. And
(c)
is due to
\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{h}
holds.

We assume the sequence of updating episodes
1=k_{1}\leq k_{2}\leq\dots\leq k_{N_{K}}\leq K
, such that the latest update episode before
k
is
k_{\mathrm{last}}=k_{N_{k}}
. Then for all
k\in[K]
, we have

\displaystyle Q^{k}_{h+1}(\cdot,\cdot)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\min_{i\leq N_{k}}\left\{\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot),\bm{\theta}_{k_{i}-1,h}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k_{i}-1,h}\rangle+\beta_{R,k_{i}-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k_{i}-1,h}^{-1}}+\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{i}-1,h}^{-1}},\mathcal{H}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq{}

\displaystyle Q^{*}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot),

which implies the case of
h
is true.

The proof for pessimism inequality is similar to the optimism.
∎

H.6
Proof of Lemma
F.6

Proof of Lemma
F.6
.

First,
\left|\left[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}-\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|\leq\mathcal{H}^{2}
since both
\left[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}\right](\cdot,\cdot)
and
\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](\cdot,\cdot)
are bounded in
[0,\mathcal{H}^{2}]
. Then it follows that

\displaystyle\left|\left[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}-\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left|\left[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}-\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|+\left|\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}-\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|.

We next bound the two terms in the RHS of the last inequality separately.

Bound the first term.

It follows that

\displaystyle\left|\left[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}-\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\right\rangle_{[0,\mathcal{H}^{2}]}-\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\bm{w}_{h}\left[\left(V^{k}_{h+1}\right)^{2}\right]\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\right\rangle_{[0,\mathcal{H}]}^{2}-\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\rangle^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[\left(V^{k}_{h+1}\right)^{2}\right]\right\rangle\right|+2\mathcal{H}\left|\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\left\|\widetilde{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[\left(V^{k}_{h+1}\right)^{2}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathcal{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 3\mathcal{H}\beta_{0}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}},

where
(a)
is due to both
\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\right\rangle_{[0,\mathcal{H}]}
and
\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\rangle
are bounded in
[0,\mathcal{H}]
.
(b)
holds due to Cauchy-Schwartz inequality and
(c)
is due to
\mathcal{E}_{0}
holds.

Bound the second term.

We have

\displaystyle\left|\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}-\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left|\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}\right)^{2}-\left(V^{*}_{h+1}\right)^{2}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|+\left|\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}\right]^{2}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})-\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right]^{2}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\left|\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}+V^{*}_{h+1}\right)\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}+V^{*}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 4\mathcal{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 4\mathcal{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-\check{V}^{k}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 4\mathcal{H}\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\check{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+4\mathcal{H}\left(\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}+\left\|\check{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}\right)\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 4\mathcal{H}\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\check{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\rangle+8\mathcal{H}\beta_{0}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}},

where
(a)
holds due to
V^{k}_{h+1}(\cdot),V^{*}_{h+1}(\cdot)
are both bounded in
[0,\mathcal{H}]
,
\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{h+1}
holds and the optimism by Lemma
F.5
,
(b)
holds due to the pessimism by Lemma
F.5
,
(c)
holds due to Cauchy-Schwartz inequality and
(d)
is due to
\mathcal{E}_{0}
holds.

Putting pieces together, we have

\left|\left[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}-\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|\leq 4\mathcal{H}\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\check{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\rangle+11\mathcal{H}\beta_{0}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}.

∎

H.7
Proof of Lemma
F.7

Proof of Lemma
F.7
.

First,
\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\leq\mathcal{H}^{2}
since both
V^{k}_{h+1}(\cdot)
and
V^{*}_{h+1}(\cdot)
are bounded in
[0,\mathcal{H}]
. Then, for any
i\leq k
, we have

\displaystyle\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right)^{2}\right](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-\check{V}^{k}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{i}_{h+1}-\check{V}^{i}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{H}\langle\bm{\phi}_{i,h},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{i-1,h}-\check{\bm{w}}_{i-1,h}\rangle+4\mathcal{H}\beta_{0}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{i-1,h}^{-1}},

where
(a)
holds due to both
V^{k}_{h+1},V^{*}_{h+1}
are bounded in
[0,\mathcal{H}]
,
\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{h+1}
holds and the optimism by Lemma
F.5
,
(b)
holds due to the pessimism by Lemma
F.5
,
(c)
holds due to
V^{k}_{h+1}
is non-increasing and
\check{V}^{k}_{h+1}
is non-decreasing by definition and
(d)
is due to Cauchy-Schwartz inequality and
\mathcal{E}_{0}
holds.

The proof of inequality for
\check{V}^{k}_{h+1}
is similar to the proof of
V^{k}_{h+1}
above.
∎

H.8
Proof of Lemma
F.8

Proof of Lemma
F.8
.

We prove Lemma
F.8
by induction. First, when
h=H
, event
\mathcal{E}_{H}
holds since
V^{k}_{H+1}(\cdot)=V^{*}_{H+1}(\cdot)=\check{V}^{k}_{H+1}(\cdot)
= 0 for all
k\in[H]
. Then, we prove with probability at least
1-\frac{2\delta}{H}
,
\mathcal{E}_{h}
holds on event
\mathcal{E}_{h+1}
. By induction over
h
, with probability at least
1-2\delta
, on event
\mathcal{E}_{0}\cap\mathcal{E}_{R}
, event
\mathcal{E}_{V}=\mathcal{E}_{1}
holds.

Note
\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}=\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]=\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\left[V^{*}_{h+1}\right]+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right]
and
\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]=\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{*}_{h+1}\right]+\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right]
. It follows that

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\left[V^{*}_{h+1}\right]-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{*}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}+\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right]-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}.

Next, we bound the two terms in the RHS by Lemma
I.5
separately.

Bound the first term.

The first condition of Lemma
I.5
is satisfied since
V^{*}_{h+1}
is a deterministic function. We have
C_{0}=\mathcal{H}
and
\mathcal{A}_{k,h}=\left\{\sigma_{k,h}^{2}\geq\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right\}
is
\mathcal{F}_{k,h}
-measurable. Since
\mathcal{E}_{0}\cap\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{h+1}
holds, for all
k\in[K]
,
\left|\left[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}-\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|\leq E_{k,h}
by Lemma
F.6
. Thus,
\sigma_{k,h}^{2}\geq\left[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+E_{k,h}\geq\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})
for all
k\in[K]
, which implies
\bigcap_{k\in[K]}\mathcal{A}_{k,h}
holds and thus
C_{\sigma}=1
. By Lemma
I.5
, with probability at least
1-\delta/H
, for all
k\in[K]
,

\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\left[V^{*}_{h+1}\right]-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{*}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}\leq\beta_{1},

where

\displaystyle\beta_{1}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{d\lambda_{V}}\mathcal{H}+8\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{K}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}d\lambda_{V}}\right)\log\frac{4HK^{2}}{\delta}}+\frac{4}{d^{2.5}H}\log\frac{4HK^{2}}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{d\lambda_{V}}\mathcal{H}+8\sqrt{d}\iota_{1}+4\iota_{1}.

Bound the second term.

The second condition of Lemma
I.5
is satisfied since
V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}
are random functions. We have
C_{0}=\mathcal{H}
and
\mathcal{A}_{k,h}=\left\{\sigma_{k,h}^{2}\geq d^{3}H\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h}-V^{*}_{h}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right\}
is
\mathcal{F}_{k,h}
-measurable. Since
\mathcal{E}_{0}\cap\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{h+1}
holds, for all
k\in[K]
,
\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h}-V^{*}_{h}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\leq D_{k,h}
by Lemma
F.7
. Thus, by definition of
\sigma_{k,h}
, we have
\bigcap_{k\in[K]}\mathcal{A}_{k,h}
holds and thus
C_{\sigma}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d^{3}H}}
. And the log-covering number of the function class that covers
V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}
can be bounded by Lemma
I.9
as
\log N_{0}=|\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V}^{+},\epsilon_{0})|\leq 6d^{3}H\iota_{1}^{2}
with
\mathcal{V}^{+}
defined in (
I.2
) and
\epsilon_{0}=\min\left\{\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{\sqrt{d^{3}H}},\frac{\lambda_{V}\mathcal{H}\sqrt{d}}{K}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\right\}
. By Lemma
I.5
, with probability at least
1-\delta/H
, for all
k\in[K]
,

\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right]-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{*}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}\leq\beta_{2},

where

\displaystyle\beta_{2}

\displaystyle=2\sqrt{d\lambda_{V}}\mathcal{H}+\frac{32}{\sqrt{d^{3}H}}\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{K}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}d\lambda_{V}}\right)\log\frac{4N_{0}HK^{2}}{\delta}}+\frac{4}{d^{2.5}H}\log\frac{4N_{0}HK^{2}}{\delta}

\displaystyle=O(\sqrt{d\lambda_{V}}\mathcal{H}+\sqrt{d}\iota_{1}^{2}).

The proof for the pessimism is similar to that of the optimism.

Finally, putting pieces together and we have
\beta_{V}=\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}=O(\sqrt{d\lambda_{V}}\mathcal{H}+\sqrt{d}\iota_{1}^{2})
.

∎

H.9
Proof of Lemma
F.9

Proof of Lemma
F.9
.

For any
k\in[K]
, recall
k_{\mathrm{last}}
is the latest update episode before episode
k
satisfying
k_{\mathrm{last}}\leq k
and
V^{k}_{h}(\cdot)=V^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}_{h}(\cdot),\forall h\in[H]
.
By Lemma
I.7
, due to
\bm{H}_{k-1,h}\succeq\bm{H}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}
and
\det(\bm{H}_{k-1,h})\leq 2\det(\bm{H}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h})
by the updating rule, it follows that for any
\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\|\bm{x}\|_{\bm{H}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}\leq 2\|\bm{x}\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}.

(H.5)

Telescoping on
V_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h})-V_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h})
.

By definition,
Q_{h}^{k}(\cdot,\cdot)\leq\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot),\bm{\theta}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}\rangle+\beta_{R,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}+\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}
and
Q_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(\cdot,\cdot)=\left\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot),\bm{\theta}^{*}_{h}+\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1}\right]\right\rangle
.
Since
a_{k,h}=\pi_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h})=\mathop{\mathrm{argmax}}_{a\in\mathcal{A}}Q_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h},a)
, we have

\displaystyle V_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h})-V_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle Q_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})-Q_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\left(\bm{\theta}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{\theta}_{h}^{*}\right)+\left(\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1}\right]\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+\beta_{R,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}+\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\left(\bm{\theta}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{\theta}_{h}^{*}\right)+\left(\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}_{h+1}\right]\right)+\left(\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1}\right]\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+2\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+2\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+4\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+4\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

Here
(a)
uses (
H.5
),
(b)
uses

\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\left(\bm{\theta}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{\theta}_{h}^{*}\right)+\left(\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}_{h+1}\right]\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}\left\|\bm{\theta}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{\theta}_{h}^{*}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\beta_{R,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}+\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 2\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+2\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

on
\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{V}
and

\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1}\right]\right\rangle=\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h}).

We define
X_{k,h}:=\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})-\left[V^{k}_{h+1}(s_{k,h+1})-V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1}(s_{k,h+1})\right]
. Then it follows that

\displaystyle V_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h})-V_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle V^{k}_{h+1}(s_{k,h+1})-V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1}(s_{k,h+1})+X_{k,h}+4\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+4\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}.

Summing the inequality above over
h^{\prime}
such that
h\leq h^{\prime}\leq H
and we have

V_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h})-V_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h})\leq\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\left[X_{k,h^{\prime}}+4\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h^{\prime}}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h^{\prime}}^{-1}}+4\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h^{\prime}}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h^{\prime}}^{-1}}\right].

(H.6)

Therefore, setting
h=1
and summing (
H.6
) over
k\in[K]
, we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[V_{1}^{k}(s_{k,h})-V_{1}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h})\right]\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[X_{k,h}+4\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+4\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\right].

(H.7)

Bound
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{k,h}
.

Notice
X_{k,h}
is
\mathcal{F}_{k,h+1}
-measurable with
\mathbb{E}[X_{k,h}|\mathcal{F}_{k,h}]=0
,
|X_{k,h}|\leq 2\mathcal{H}
and

\mathbb{E}[X_{k,h}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{k,h}]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left[V_{h+1}^{k}(s_{k,h+1})-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h+1})\right]^{2}\bigg|\mathcal{F}_{k,h}\right]\overset{(a)}{\leq}\mathcal{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h}),

where
(a)
holds due to
\left|V_{h+1}^{k}(\cdot)-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}(\cdot)\right|\leq\mathcal{H}
and optimism in Lemma
F.5

By the variance-aware Freedman inequality in Lemma
I.2
, with probability at least
1-\frac{\delta}{2}
, it follows that

\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{k,h}\right|\leq 3\sqrt{\iota}\sqrt{\mathcal{H}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})}+10\mathcal{H}\iota

(H.8)

where
\iota=\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}
.

Bound
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})
.

Notice

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}\left[V_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h})-V_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h})\right]+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{k,h}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\left[X_{k,h^{\prime}}+4\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h^{\prime}}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h^{\prime}}^{-1}}+4\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h^{\prime}}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h^{\prime}}^{-1}}\right]+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{k,h}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}(H-h+1)\left[X_{k,h}+4\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+4\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\right]+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{k,h}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{k,h}b_{h}+4H\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+4H\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

where
(a)
uses (
H.6
) and
(b)
uses the notation

b_{h}=\begin{cases}1&\text{if }h=1,\\
H-h+2&\text{if }2\leq h\leq H.\end{cases}

Since
|b_{h}|\leq H
, by the variance-aware Freedman inequality in Lemma
I.2
, with probability at least
1-\frac{\delta}{2}
, we have

\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{k,h}b_{h}\right|\leq 3H\sqrt{\iota}\sqrt{\mathcal{H}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})}+10H\mathcal{H}\iota.

Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

(H.9)

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 3H\sqrt{\iota}\sqrt{\mathcal{H}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})}+10H\mathcal{H}\iota

\displaystyle+4H\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+4H\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 8H\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+8H\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+29H^{2}\mathcal{H}\iota,

where
(a)
holds since
x\leq a\sqrt{x}+b
implies
x\leq a^{2}+2b
.

Putting pieces together.

Finally, plug-in (
H.7
), and we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[V_{1}^{k}(s_{k,1})-V_{1}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[X_{k,h}+4\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+4\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:final1}}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 3\sqrt{\iota}\sqrt{\mathcal{H}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})}+10\mathcal{H}\iota

\displaystyle\qquad+4\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+4\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:final-expect-sum}}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 3\sqrt{\iota}\sqrt{\mathcal{H}\cdot\left[8H\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+8H\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+29H^{2}\mathcal{H}\iota\right]}

\displaystyle\qquad+4\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+4\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+10\mathcal{H}\iota

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 6\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+6\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+36H\mathcal{H}\iota

where the last inequality uses
\sqrt{a+b}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}
and
2\sqrt{ab}\leq a+b
for non-negative numbers
a,b\geq 0
.
∎

H.10
Proof of Lemma
F.10

Proof of Lemma
F.10
.

For any
k\in[K]
, recall
k_{\mathrm{last}}
is the latest update episode before episode
k
satisfying
k_{\mathrm{last}}\leq k
and
V^{k}_{h}(\cdot)=V^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}_{h}(\cdot),\check{V}^{k}_{h}(\cdot)=\check{V}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}_{h}(\cdot),\forall h\in[H]
.

By definition,
Q_{h}^{k}(\cdot,\cdot)\leq\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot),\bm{\theta}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}+\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}\rangle+\beta_{R,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}+\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}
and
\check{Q}_{h}^{k}(\cdot,\cdot)\geq\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,\cdot),\bm{\theta}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}+\check{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}\rangle-\beta_{R,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}-\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}
.
Since
a_{k,h}=\pi_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h})=\mathop{\mathrm{argmax}}_{a\in\mathcal{A}}Q_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h},a)
, we have

\displaystyle V_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h})-\check{V}_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h})

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle Q_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})-\check{Q}_{h}^{k}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\check{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}\right\rangle+2\beta_{R,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}+2\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\left(\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}_{h+1}\right]\right)-\left(\check{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[\check{V}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}_{h+1}\right]\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}-\check{V}^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\rangle+4\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+4\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-\check{V}^{k}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+4\beta_{R,k-1}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+8\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

Here
(a)
uses (
H.5
),
(b)
uses

\displaystyle\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\left(\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}_{h+1}\right]\right)-\left(\check{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[\check{V}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}_{h+1}\right]\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}\left\|\check{\bm{w}}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}-\bm{w}_{h}\left[\check{V}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}_{h+1}\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 2\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{\mathrm{last}}-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 4\beta_{V}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

on
\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{V}
and

\left\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\bm{w}_{h}\left[V^{k}_{h+1}-\check{V}^{k}_{h+1}\right]\right\rangle=\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-\check{V}^{k}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h}).

The rest of the proof is nearly the same as that in Lemma
F.9
except for the constants, and we omit it to avoid repetition.
∎

H.11
Proof of Lemma
F.11

Proof of Lemma
F.11
.

Notice

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\nu_{k,h}w_{k,h}

where
w_{k,h}=\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}/\nu_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}}
.
And we have
w_{k,h}\leq c_{0}\leq 1
by definition of
\nu_{k,h},c_{0}
and thus for all
h\in[H]
,

\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k,h}^{2}=\min\left\{1,w_{k,h}^{2}\right\}\leq 2\kappa

(H.10)

by Lemma
I.6
, where
\kappa
is defined in Algorithm
3
.

Next we bound the sum of bonus
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\nu_{k,h}w_{k,h}
separately by the value of
\nu_{k,h}
. Recall
\nu_{k,h}
is defined in (
5.4
). We decompose
[K]\times[H]
as the union of three disjoint sets
\mathcal{J}_{1},\mathcal{J}_{2},\mathcal{J}_{3}
where

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{1}

\displaystyle=\left\{(k,h)\in[K]\times[H]|\nu_{k,h}\in\left\{\widehat{\nu}_{k,h},\nu_{\mathrm{min}}\right\}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\{(k,h)\in[K]\times[H]\bigg|\nu_{k,h}=\frac{\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}}{c_{0}}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{3}

\displaystyle=\left\{(k,h)\in[K]\times[H]\Bigg|\nu_{k,h}=\frac{\sqrt{\max\left\{B,W\right\}}}{c_{1}^{\frac{1}{4}}(2\kappa)^{\frac{1}{4}}}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}.

For the summation over
\mathcal{J}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{(k,h)\in\mathcal{J}_{1}}\nu_{k,h}w_{k,h}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(k,h)\in\mathcal{J}_{1}}\max\left\{\widehat{\nu}_{k,h},\nu_{\mathrm{min}}\right\}w_{k,h}

(H.11)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}(\widehat{\nu}_{k,h}^{2}+\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2})}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}w_{k,h}^{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\sqrt{2H\kappa}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\widehat{\nu}_{k,h}^{2}+HK\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}},

where
(a)
holds due to Cauchy-Schwartz inequality and
(b)
holds due to (
H.10
).

We provide a upper bound for
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\widehat{\nu}_{k,h}^{2}
in Lemma
H.1
.

Lemma H.1
.

With probability at least
1-\delta
, on event
\mathcal{E}_{R^{\epsilon}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{R}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\widehat{\nu}_{k,h}^{2}=O\Bigg(

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}^{*}K+\left(\beta_{R^{\epsilon}}+\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}\beta_{R}\kappa\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}

\displaystyle+H\nu_{R}^{2}\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}\Bigg).

Proof.

See Appendix
H.13
for a detailed proof.
∎

Next, for the summation over
\mathcal{J}_{2}
, we have
\left\|\frac{\bm{\phi}_{k,h}}{\nu_{k,h}}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=c_{0}
. Then

\sum_{(k,h)\in\mathcal{J}_{2}}\nu_{k,h}w_{k,h}=\frac{1}{c_{0}^{2}}\sum_{(k,h)\in\mathcal{J}_{2}}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}w_{k,h}^{2}\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{1}{c_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\lambda_{R}}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}w_{k,h}^{2}\overset{(b)}{\leq}\frac{2H\kappa}{c_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\lambda_{R}}},

(H.12)

where
(a)
holds due to
\bm{H}_{k-1,h}\succeq\lambda_{R}\bm{I}
, thus
\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\leq\frac{\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|}{\sqrt{\lambda_{R}}}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{R}}}
. And
(b)
holds due to (
H.10
).

Then, for the summation over
\mathcal{J}_{3}
, we have
\nu_{k,h}=\frac{\max\left\{B,W\right\}}{\sqrt{2c_{1}\kappa}}w_{k,h}
. Therefore

\sum_{(k,h)\in\mathcal{J}_{3}}\nu_{k,h}w_{k,h}=\frac{\max\left\{B,W\right\}}{\sqrt{2c_{1}\kappa}}\sum_{(k,h)\in\mathcal{J}_{3}}w_{k,h}^{2}\leq\frac{\max\left\{B,W\right\}H\sqrt{2\kappa}}{\sqrt{c_{1}}},

(H.13)

where the inequality holds due to (
H.10
).

Finally, combining (
H.11
), (
H.12
), (
H.13
) and Lemma
H.1
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{H\kappa}\left(\beta_{R^{\epsilon}}+\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}\beta_{R}\kappa\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\right)^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}}+C\right)

where

C=\sqrt{H\kappa}\sqrt{\mathcal{U}^{*}K+HK\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}+H\nu_{R}^{2}\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}}+\frac{H\kappa}{c_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\lambda_{R}}}+\frac{\max\left\{B,W\right\}H\sqrt{\kappa}}{\sqrt{c_{1}}}.

Using the inequality that
x\leq ax^{\frac{1}{1+\epsilon}}+b
implies
x\leq a^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{\epsilon}}+\frac{1+\epsilon}{\epsilon}b
, we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=O\left((H\kappa)^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2\epsilon}}\left(\beta_{R^{\epsilon}}+\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}\beta_{R}\kappa\right)^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}+C\right).

Next, we simplify the expression above by hiding logarithmic terms. Notice
\kappa=\widetilde{O}(d)
. Setting
\lambda_{R}=\frac{d}{\max\left\{B^{2},W^{2}\right\}}
, we have
\beta_{R^{\epsilon}}=\widetilde{O}\left(\sqrt{d}\frac{\nu_{R^{\epsilon}}}{\nu_{\mathrm{min}}}K^{\frac{1-\epsilon^{\prime}}{2(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}}\right)
and
\beta_{R}=\widetilde{O}\left(\sqrt{d}K^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\right)
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle=O(C)+\widetilde{O}\left(\left(d^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}H^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}\beta_{R^{\epsilon}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}+\left(d^{\frac{3+\epsilon}{2}}H^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}\beta_{R}\right)^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}\right)

\displaystyle=O(C)+\widetilde{O}\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{\nu_{R^{\epsilon}}d^{\frac{2+\epsilon}{2}}H^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}K^{\frac{1-\epsilon^{\prime}}{2(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}}}{\nu_{\mathrm{min}}}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}+\left(d^{\frac{4+\epsilon}{2}}H^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}K^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}\Bigg).

We then simplify
C
as

C=\widetilde{O}\left(\sqrt{dH\mathcal{U}^{*}K}+\sqrt{dH^{2}K\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}}+\left(\nu_{R}+\max\left\{B,W\right\}\right)\sqrt{d}H\right).

Finally, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=\widetilde{O}\Bigg(

\displaystyle\sqrt{dH\mathcal{U}^{*}K}+\sqrt{dH^{2}K\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}}+\Bigg(\frac{\nu_{R^{\epsilon}}d^{\frac{2+\epsilon}{2}}H^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}K^{\frac{1-\epsilon^{\prime}}{2(1+\epsilon^{\prime})}}}{\nu_{\mathrm{min}}}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}

\displaystyle+\left(d^{\frac{4+\epsilon}{2}}H^{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2}}\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}K^{\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(1+\epsilon)}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\epsilon}}+\left(\nu_{R}+\max\left\{B,W\right\}\right)\sqrt{d}H\Bigg).

∎

H.12
Proof of Lemma
F.12

Proof of Lemma
F.12
.

Denote
b_{k,h}=\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}/\sigma_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}}
, then

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sigma_{k,h}b_{k,h}.

And we have
b_{k,h}\leq 1
by definition of
\sigma_{k,h}
and thus for all
h\in[H]
,

\sum_{k=1}^{K}b_{k,h}^{2}=\min\left\{1,b_{k,h}^{2}\right\}\leq 2\kappa_{V}

(H.14)

by Lemma
I.6
, where
\kappa_{V}:=d\log\left(1+\frac{K}{d\lambda_{V}\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}}\right)
.

Next we bound the sum of bonus
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sigma_{k,h}b_{k,h}
separately by the value of
\sigma_{k,h}
. Recall
\sigma_{k,h}
is defined in (
5.9
). We decompose
[K]\times[H]
as the union of three disjoint sets
\mathcal{J}_{1},\mathcal{J}_{2},\mathcal{J}_{3}
where

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{1}

\displaystyle=\left\{(k,h)\in[K]\times[H]\bigg|\sigma_{k,h}\in\left\{\widehat{\sigma}_{k,h},\sqrt{d^{3}HD_{k,h}},\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}\right\}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\{(k,h)\in[K]\times[H]\big|\sigma_{k,h}=\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{3}

\displaystyle=\left\{(k,h)\in[K]\times[H]\bigg|\sigma_{k,h}=\sqrt{d^{\frac{5}{2}}H\mathcal{H}}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}.

For the summation over
\mathcal{J}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{(k,h)\in\mathcal{J}_{1}}\sigma_{k,h}b_{k,h}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(k,h)\in\mathcal{J}_{1}}\max\left\{\widehat{\sigma}_{k,h},\sqrt{d^{3}HD_{k,h}},\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}\right\}\cdot b_{k,h}

(H.15)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}(\widehat{\sigma}_{k,h}^{2}+d^{3}HD_{k,h}+\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}^{2})}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}b_{k,h}^{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\sqrt{2H\kappa_{V}}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\widehat{\sigma}_{k,h}^{2}+d^{3}H\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}D_{k,h}+HK\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}},

where
(a)
holds due to Cauchy-Schwartz inequality and
(b)
holds due to (
H.14
).

We provide upper bounds for
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\widehat{\sigma}_{k,h}^{2}
and
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}D_{k,h}
in Lemma
H.2
and Lemma
H.3
respectively.

Lemma H.2
.

With probability at least
1-2\delta
, on event
\mathcal{E}_{0}\cap\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{V}\cap\mathcal{A}_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\widehat{\sigma}_{k,h}^{2}=O\bigg(

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}^{*}K+(\beta_{0}+H\beta_{V})\mathcal{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+H\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+H^{2}\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}\bigg).

Proof.

See Appendix
H.14
for a detailed proof.
∎

Lemma H.3
.

On event
\mathcal{E}_{0}\cap\mathcal{A}_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}D_{k,h}=O\bigg(
