\displaystyle(\beta_{0}+H\beta_{V})\mathcal{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+H\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle+H^{2}\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}\bigg).

Proof.

See Appendix
H.15
for a detailed proof.
∎

Next, for the summation over
\mathcal{J}_{2}
, we have
\left\|\frac{\bm{\phi}_{k,h}}{\sigma_{k,h}}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=1
. Then

\sum_{(k,h)\in\mathcal{J}_{2}}\sigma_{k,h}b_{k,h}=\sum_{(k,h)\in\mathcal{J}_{2}}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}b_{k,h}^{2}\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{V}}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}b_{k,h}^{2}\overset{(b)}{\leq}\frac{2H\kappa_{V}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{V}}},

(H.16)

where
(a)
holds due to
\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}\succeq\lambda_{V}\bm{I}
, thus
\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\leq\frac{\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|}{\sqrt{\lambda_{V}}}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{V}}}
. And
(b)
holds due to (
H.14
).

Then, for the summation over
\mathcal{J}_{3}
, we have
\sigma_{k,h}=d^{2.5}H\mathcal{H}b_{k,h}
. Therefore

\sum_{(k,h)\in\mathcal{J}_{3}}\sigma_{k,h}b_{k,h}=d^{2.5}H\mathcal{H}\sum_{(k,h)\in\mathcal{J}_{3}}b_{k,h}^{2}\leq 2d^{2.5}H^{2}\mathcal{H}\kappa_{V},

(H.17)

where the inequality holds due to (
H.14
).
∎

Finally, combining (
H.15
), (
H.16
), (
H.17
) and Lemma
H.2
, with probability at least
1-2\delta
, we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=O\left(\sqrt{H\kappa_{V}}\sqrt{(\beta_{0}+H\beta_{V})d^{3}H\mathcal{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}}+C\right)

where

\displaystyle C=

\displaystyle\sqrt{H\kappa_{V}}\sqrt{\mathcal{V}^{*}K+HK\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}+d^{3}H^{2}\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+d^{3}H^{3}\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}}

\displaystyle+\frac{H\kappa_{V}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{V}}}+d^{2.5}H^{2}\mathcal{H}\kappa_{V}.

Using the inequality that
x\leq a\sqrt{x}+b
implies
x\leq a^{2}+2b
, we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=O\left((\beta_{0}+H\beta_{V})d^{3}H^{2}\mathcal{H}\kappa_{V}+C\right).

Next, we simplify the expression above by hiding logarithmic terms. Notice
\kappa_{V}=\widetilde{O}(d)
. Setting
\lambda_{V}=\frac{1}{\mathcal{H}^{2}}
, we have
\beta_{0}=\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d^{3}H\mathcal{H}^{2}}}{\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}}\right)
and
\beta_{V}=\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{d})
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle=O(C)+\widetilde{O}\left(d^{4}H^{2}\mathcal{H}\beta_{0}+d^{4}H^{3}\mathcal{H}\beta_{V}\right)

\displaystyle=O(C)+\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{d^{5.5}H^{2.5}\mathcal{H}^{2}}{\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}}+d^{4.5}H^{3}\mathcal{H}\right).

We then simplify
C
as

C=\widetilde{O}\left(\sqrt{dH\mathcal{V}^{*}K}+\sqrt{dH^{2}K\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}}+\sqrt{d^{4}H^{3}\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}}+d^{2.5}H^{2}\mathcal{H}\right).

Finally, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}=\widetilde{O}\Bigg(

\displaystyle\sqrt{dH\mathcal{V}^{*}K}+\sqrt{dH^{2}K\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}}+\frac{d^{5.5}H^{2.5}\mathcal{H}^{2}}{\sigma_{\mathrm{min}}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{d^{4}H^{3}\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}}+d^{4.5}H^{3}\mathcal{H}\bigg).

H.13
Proof of Lemma
H.1

Proof of Lemma
H.1
.

On event
\mathcal{E}_{R^{\epsilon}}\cap\mathcal{E}_{R}
, by Lemma
F.2
, for all
(k,h)\in[K]\times[H]
, we have
\left|\left[\widehat{\bm{\nu}}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}-\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|\leq W_{k,h}
. Thus

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\widehat{\nu}_{k,h}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\left[\widehat{\bm{\nu}}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+W_{k,h}\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\left[\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+2W_{k,h}\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}2\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right]^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+2\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}W_{k,h}^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}},

where
(a)
holds due to Jensen’s inequality: for any non-negative number
a,b
, we have
(a+b)^{1+\xi}\leq 2^{\xi}a+2^{\xi}b\leq 2a+2b
for
\xi\in[0,1]
.
We next bound the two terms in the RHS separately.

For the first term.

On one hand, we have
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right]^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\leq\mathcal{U}K
by Assumption
2.7
.

On the other hand, we denote
X_{k}=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right]^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})
. Use the notation
\mathcal{F}_{k}:=\mathcal{F}_{H,k}
as the
\sigma
-field generated by all the random variables up to
k
-th episode for short. Then
X_{k}\geq 0
is
\mathcal{F}_{k}
-measurable with
|X_{k}|\leq H\nu_{R}^{2}
and
\Var[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]\leq\mathbb{E}[X_{k}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]\leq H\nu_{R}^{2}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]
. With probability at least
1-\delta
, the variance-aware Freedman inequality in Lemma
I.2
gives

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}X_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]+3\sqrt{H\nu_{R}^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}}+5H\nu_{R}^{2}\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]+7H\nu_{R}^{2}\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}.

We relate
\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]
to policy
\pi^{k}
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right]^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{(s_{h},a_{h})\sim d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right]^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}(s_{h},a_{h})\right],

where
d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(s,a)
is defined in (
F.7
).
Then we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right]^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 3\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{(s_{h},a_{h})\sim d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\bm{\nu}_{1+\epsilon}R_{h}\right]^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}(s_{h},a_{h})\right]+7H\nu_{R}^{2}\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle 3\mathcal{U}^{*}_{0}K+7H\nu_{R}^{2}\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta},

where
\mathcal{U}^{*}_{0}
is defined in (
F.5
).

For the second term.

Recall
W_{k,h}
is defined in (
5.6
). We have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}W_{k,h}^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[(\beta_{R^{\epsilon},k-1}+6\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}\beta_{R,k-1}\kappa)\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\right]^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}

\displaystyle\leq(\beta_{R^{\epsilon}}+6\mathcal{H}^{\epsilon}\beta_{R}\kappa)^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\right)^{\frac{2}{1+\epsilon}},

where the inequality holds due to Jensen’s inequality.

Finally, putting pieces together and using
\mathcal{U}^{*}=\min\left\{\mathcal{U}^{*}_{0},\mathcal{U}\right\}
complete the proof.
∎

H.14
Proof of Lemma
H.2

Proof of Lemma
H.2
.

On event
\mathcal{E}_{0}\cap\mathcal{E}_{R}\cap\mathcal{E}_{V}
, by Lemma
F.6
, for all
(k,h)\in[K]\times[H]
, we have

\left|\left[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}-\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right|\leq E_{k,h}
. Thus

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\widehat{\sigma}_{k,h}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(\left[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V^{k}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+E_{k,h}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+2\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}E_{k,h}.

We next bound the two terms in the RHS separately.

For the first term.

On one hand, we denote
X_{k}=\sum_{h=1}^{H}[\mathbb{V}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})
.
Use the notation
\mathcal{F}_{k}:=\mathcal{F}_{k,h}
as the
\sigma
-field generated by all the random variables up to
k
-th episodes for short.
Then
X_{k}\geq 0
is
\mathcal{F}_{k}
-measurable with
|X_{k}|\leq H\mathcal{H}^{2}
and
\Var[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]\leq\mathbb{E}[X_{k}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]\leq H\mathcal{H}^{2}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]
.
With probability at least
1-\delta
, the variance-aware Freedman inequality in Lemma
I.2
gives

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}X_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]+3\sqrt{H\mathcal{H}^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}}+5H\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]+7H\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}.

We relate
\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]
to
\pi^{k}
as

\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{(s_{h},a_{h})\sim d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}\right](s_{h},a_{h})\right],

where
d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(s,a)
is defined in (
F.7
).
Then we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 3\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{(s_{h},a_{h})\sim d_{h}^{\pi^{k}}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{h},a_{h})\right]+7H\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle 3\mathcal{V}^{*}_{0}K+7H\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta},

where
\mathcal{V}^{*}_{0}
is defined in (
F.6
).

We restate the total variance lemma (Lemma C.5 in
Jin et al. 2018
) here for completeness.

Lemma H.4

(Total variance lemma)
.

With probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}[\mathbb{V}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\leq 2\mathcal{V}K+2H\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}.

Proof.

See Appendix
H.16
for a detailed proof.
∎

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{*}_{h+1}-\mathbb{V}_{h}V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{V}K+2H\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}+2\mathcal{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-\check{V}^{k}_{h+1}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{V}K+2H\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}+16H\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+16H\mathcal{H}\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+58H^{2}\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{V}K+16H\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+16H\mathcal{H}\beta_{V}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+60H^{2}\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta},

where
(a)
holds with probability at least
1-\delta
due to Lemma
H.4

and (
H.18
).
(b)
is due to
\mathcal{A}_{0}
holds.

\displaystyle\left[\mathbb{V}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}-\mathbb{V}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

(H.18)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle[\mathbb{P}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}]^{2}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}]^{2}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle-\left([\mathbb{P}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}]^{2}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}]^{2}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle[\mathbb{P}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}]^{2}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})-\mathbb{P}_{h}[V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}]^{2}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{*}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h}),

where
(a)
holds due to
V_{h+1}^{*}(\cdot)\geq V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}(\cdot)
.
(b)
holds due to
V_{h+1}^{*}(\cdot)
and
V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}
are bounded in
[0,\mathcal{H}]
. And
(c)
holds due to Lemma
F.5
.

For the second term.

Recall
E_{k,h}
is defined in (
5.11
). We have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}E_{k,h}\leq{}

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[4\mathcal{H}\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\check{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\rangle+11\mathcal{H}\beta_{0}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[4\mathcal{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-\check{V}^{k}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+22\mathcal{H}\beta_{0}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle(22\beta_{0}+48H\beta_{V})\mathcal{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+32H\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+116H^{2}\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle O\Bigg((\beta_{0}+H\beta_{V})\mathcal{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+H\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+H^{2}\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}\Bigg),

where
(a)
is due to
\mathcal{E}_{0}
holds and
(b)
is due to
\mathcal{A}_{0}
holds.

Finally, putting pieces together and using
\mathcal{V}^{*}=\min\left\{\mathcal{V}^{*}_{0},\mathcal{V}\right\}
completes the proof.
∎

H.15
Proof of Lemma
H.3

Proof of Lemma
H.3
.

Recall
D_{k,h}
is defined in (
5.12
). We have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}D_{k,h}\leq{}

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[2\mathcal{H}\langle\bm{\phi}_{k,h},\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}-\check{\bm{w}}_{k-1,h}\rangle+4\mathcal{H}\beta_{0}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[2\mathcal{H}\left[\mathbb{P}_{h}\left(V^{k}_{h+1}-\check{V}^{k}_{h+1}\right)\right](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+8\mathcal{H}\beta_{0}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}{}

\displaystyle(8\beta_{0}+24H\beta_{V})\mathcal{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+16H\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+58H^{2}\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle O\Bigg((\beta_{0}+H\beta_{V})\mathcal{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}+H\mathcal{H}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left\|\bm{\phi}_{k,h}\right\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+H^{2}\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}\Bigg),

where
(a)
is due to
\mathcal{E}_{0}
holds and
(b)
is due to
\mathcal{A}_{0}
holds.
∎

H.16
Proof of Lemma
H.4

Proof of Lemma
H.4
.

The proof leverages the technique in Lemma C.5 of
Jin et al. 2018
.
We define the filtration
\mathcal{F}_{k}
as the
\sigma
-field generated by all the random variables over the first
k
episodes.
And let
X_{k}=\sum_{h=1}^{H}[\mathbb{V}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})
.
It follows that
\pi^{k}
is
\mathcal{F}_{k-1}
-measurable,
X_{k}
is
\mathcal{F}_{k}
-measurable, and
|X_{k}|\leq H\mathcal{H}^{2}
.
Let
\mathbb{E}_{k}(\cdot):=\mathbb{E}[\cdot|\mathcal{F}_{k}]
for short.
We have

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\geq}\Var\left(\sum_{h=1}^{H}r_{h}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{k-1}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}r_{h}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})-V_{1}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,1})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}\mathbb{E}_{k-1}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(r_{h}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h+1})-V_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h})\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{k-1}\left[r_{h}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h+1})-V_{h}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{k-1}\left[V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}(s_{k,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{k-1}\sum_{h=1}^{H}[\mathbb{V}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]

where
(a)
holds due to the definition of
\mathcal{V}
,
(b)
holds due to
V_{H+1}^{\pi^{k}}(\cdot)=0
and
(c)
holds due to Markov property of MDPs.

We also note that

\Var\left(\sum_{h=1}^{H}r_{h}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right)\leq\mathcal{H}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}r_{h}(s_{k,h},a_{k,h})\right]\leq\mathcal{H}V^{*}_{1},

where the second inequality holds due to the optimality of
V^{*}_{1}
.
Thus, we actually have

\mathcal{V}\leq\mathcal{H}V^{*}_{1},

(H.19)

which shows the relationship between our result with the first-order regret.

Then
\Var[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]\leq H\mathcal{H}^{2}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]
, and we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Var[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]\leq H\mathcal{H}^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]\leq H\mathcal{H}^{2}\mathcal{V}K.

By the Freedman inequality in Lemma
I.1
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}[\mathbb{V}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}X_{k}\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}|\mathcal{F}_{k-1}]+\sqrt{2H\mathcal{H}^{2}\mathcal{V}K\log\frac{1}{\delta}}+\frac{2}{3}H\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}K+2\sqrt{H\mathcal{H}^{2}\mathcal{V}K\log\frac{1}{\delta}}+\frac{2}{3}H\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{V}K+2H\mathcal{H}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}.

∎

Appendix I
Auxiliary Lemmas

Lemma I.1

(Freedman inequality
[
13
]
)
.

Let
\{X_{t}\}_{t\in[T]}
be a stochastic process that adapts to the filtration
\mathcal{F}_{t}
so that
X_{t}
is
\mathcal{F}_{t}
-measurable,
\mathbb{E}[X_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]=0
,
|X_{t}|\leq M
and
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[X_{t}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]\leq V
where
M>0
and
V>0
are positive constants.
Then with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\sum_{t=1}^{T}X_{t}\leq\sqrt{2V\ln\frac{1}{\delta}}+\frac{2M}{3}\ln\frac{1}{\delta}.

Lemma I.2

(Variance-aware Freedman inequality, Theorem 5 in
Li et al. 2023
)
.

Let
\{X_{t}\}_{t\in[T]}
be a stochastic process that adapts to the filtration
\mathcal{F}_{t}
so that
X_{t}
is
\mathcal{F}_{t}
-measurable,
\mathbb{E}[X_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]=0
,
|X_{t}|\leq M
and
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[X_{t}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]\leq V^{2}
where
M>0
and
V>0
are positive constants.
Then with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}X_{t}\right|\leq 3\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[X_{t}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]\cdot\log\frac{2K}{\delta}}+5M\log\frac{2K}{\delta},

where
K=1+\lceil 2\log_{2}\frac{V}{M}\rceil
.

Proof of Lemma
I.2
.

By Theorem 5 in
Li et al. 2023
, we have for any positive integer
K\geq 1
,

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}X_{t}\right|\leq\sqrt{8\max\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[X_{t}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}],\frac{V^{2}}{2^{K}}\right\}\cdot\ln\frac{2K}{\delta}}+\frac{4M}{3}\ln\frac{2K}{\delta}\right)\geq 1-\delta.

By setting
K=1+\lceil 2\log_{2}\frac{V}{M}\rceil
, we have
\frac{V^{2}}{2^{K}}\leq M^{2}
.
Using
\max\{a,b\}\leq a+b
,
\sqrt{a+b}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}
for any
a,b\geq 0
and
\ln\frac{2K}{\delta}\geq 1
, we complete the proof.
∎

The following two lemmas are the counterpart lemmas of Theorem
3.3
under light-tail assumption.

Lemma I.3

(Bernstein inequality for self-normalized martingales, Lemma 4.1 in
He et al. 2023
)
.

Let
\{\mathcal{G}_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
be a filtration and
\{\bm{x}_{t},\eta_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
be a stochastic process so that
\bm{x}_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
is
\mathcal{G}_{t}
-measurable and
\eta_{t}\in\mathbb{R}
is
\mathcal{G}_{t+1}
-measurable.
If
\|\bm{x}_{t}\|\leq L
and
\{\eta_{t}\}_{t\geq 1}
satisfies that
\mathbb{E}[\eta_{t}|\mathcal{G}_{t}]=0
,
\mathbb{E}[\eta_{t}^{2}|\mathcal{G}_{t}]\leq\sigma^{2}
and
|\eta_{t}\min\left\{1,\|\bm{x}_{t}\|_{\bm{Z}_{t-1}^{-1}}\right\}|\leq M
for all
t\geq 1
.
Then, for any
\delta\in(0,1)
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have for all
t\geq 1
,

\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{t}\bm{x}_{i}\eta_{i}\right\|_{\bm{Z}_{t}^{-1}}\leq 8\sigma\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{tL^{2}}{d\lambda}\right)\log\frac{4t^{2}}{\delta}}+4M\log\frac{4t^{2}}{\delta},

where
\bm{Z}_{t}=\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{i=1}^{t}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}
for
t\geq 1
and
\bm{Z}_{0}=\lambda\bm{I}
.

Lemma I.4

(Hoeffding inequality for self-normalized martingales, Theorem 1 in
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
)
.

Let
\{\mathcal{G}_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
be a filtration and
\{\bm{x}_{t},\eta_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
be a stochastic process so that
\bm{x}_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
is
\mathcal{G}_{t}
-measurable and
\eta_{t}\in\mathbb{R}
is
\mathcal{G}_{t+1}
-measurable..
If
\|\bm{x}_{t}\|\leq L
and
\{\eta_{t}\}_{t\geq 1}
satisfies that
\mathbb{E}[\eta_{t}|\mathcal{G}_{t}]=0
and
|\eta_{t}|\leq M
for all
t\geq 1
.
Then, for any
\delta\in(0,1)
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have for all
t\geq 1
,

\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{t}\bm{x}_{i}\eta_{i}\right\|_{\bm{Z}_{t}^{-1}}\leq M\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{tL^{2}}{d\lambda}\right)+\log\frac{1}{\delta}},

where
\bm{Z}_{t}=\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{i=1}^{t}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}
for
t\geq 1
and
\bm{Z}_{0}=\lambda\bm{I}
.

The next lemma is a general result of the concentration bounds for estimated value functions, which is the core technique used in
Jin et al. 2020
,
Zhou and Gu 2022
,
He et al. 2023
. We include the proof here for completeness.

Lemma I.5
.

Fix any
h\in[H]
.
Consider a specific value function
f(\cdot)
which satisfies

(1)

\sup_{s\in\mathcal{S}}|f(s)|\leq C_{0}
;

(2)

f\in\mathcal{V}
where
\mathcal{V}
is a class of functions with
\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V},\epsilon)
the
\epsilon
-covering number of
\mathcal{V}
with respective to the distance
\mathrm{dist}(f,f^{\prime}):=\sup_{s\in\mathcal{S}}|f(s)-f^{\prime}(s)|
.

We assume there exists a deterministic
C_{\sigma}>0
and
\mathcal{A}_{k,h}
(which is
\mathcal{F}_{k,h}
-measurable) such that
\mathcal{A}_{k,h}\subseteq\left\{\sigma_{k,h}^{2}\geq[\mathbb{V}_{h}f](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})/C_{\sigma}^{2}\right\}
for all
k\in[K]
.
Let
\bm{w}_{h}[f],\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k,h}[f]
be defined in (
5.7
), (
5.7
) and
\sigma_{k,h},\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}
be defined in our algorithm.
Under any of the following conditions, with probability at least
1-\delta/H
, it follows for all
k\in[K]
,

\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k,h}[f]-\bm{w}_{h}[f]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}}\leq\beta.

(I.1)

(i)

If
f(\cdot)
is a deterministic function and
\bigcap_{k\in[K]}\mathcal{A}_{k,h}
is true, (
I.1
) holds with

\beta=\sqrt{d\lambda_{V}}C_{0}+8C_{\sigma}\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{K}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}d\lambda_{V}}\right)\log\frac{4HK^{2}}{\delta}}+\frac{4C_{0}}{d^{2.5}H\mathcal{H}}\log\frac{4HK^{2}}{\delta}.

(ii)

If
f(\cdot)
is a random function and
\bigcap_{k\in[K]}\mathcal{A}_{k,h}
is true, (
I.1
) holds with

\beta=2\sqrt{d\lambda_{V}}C_{0}+32C_{\sigma}\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{K}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}d\lambda_{V}}\right)\log\frac{4HK^{2}N_{0}}{\delta}}+\frac{4C_{0}}{d^{2.5}H\mathcal{H}}\log\frac{4HK^{2}N_{0}}{\delta}

where
N_{0}=|\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V},\epsilon_{0})|
and
\epsilon_{0}=\min\left\{C_{\sigma}\sigma_{\min},\frac{\lambda_{V}C_{0}\sqrt{d}}{K}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\right\}
.

(iii)

If
f(\cdot)
is a random function, (
I.1
) holds with

\displaystyle\beta=2\sqrt{d\lambda_{V}}C_{0}+\frac{C_{0}}{\sigma_{\min}}\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{K}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}d\lambda_{V}}\right)+\log\frac{N_{1}}{\delta}}.

where
N_{1}=|\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V},\epsilon_{1})|
and
\epsilon_{1}=\frac{\lambda_{V}C_{0}\sqrt{d}}{K}\sigma_{\min}^{2}
.

Proof.

By definition of
\bm{w}_{h}[f]
,

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k,h}[f]

\displaystyle=\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}f(s_{i,h+1})

\displaystyle=\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\left[\bm{\phi}_{i,h}^{\top}\bm{w}_{h}[f]+f(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}f](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]

\displaystyle=\bm{w}_{h}[f]-\lambda_{V}\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}\bm{w}_{h}[f]+\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\left[f(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}f](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right].

Then it follows that

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\bm{w}}_{k,h}[f]-\bm{w}_{h}[f]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\lambda_{V}\left\|\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}\bm{w}_{h}[f]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}}+\left\|\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\left[f(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}f](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\lambda_{V}\left\|\bm{w}_{h}[f]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}}+\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\left[f(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}f](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\sqrt{d\lambda_{V}}C_{0}+\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\left[f(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}f](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}},

where the last inequality holds due to
\left\|\bm{w}_{h}[f]\right\|\leq\sqrt{d}C_{0}
.

(i)

Assume
f(\cdot)
is a deterministic function.

We set
\mathcal{G}_{i}=\mathcal{F}_{i,h}
,
\bm{x}_{i}=\sigma_{i,h}^{-1}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}
,
\eta_{i}=\sigma_{i,h}^{-1}\left[f(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}f](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]
and
\bm{Z}_{k}=\lambda_{V}\bm{I}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}^{\top}=\bm{H}_{k,h}
.
Since
f(\cdot)
is deterministic,
\mathcal{A}_{k,h}\in\mathcal{F}_{k,h}
.
Clearly
\bm{x}_{i}\in\mathcal{G}_{i},\mathbb{E}[\eta_{i}|\mathcal{G}_{i}]=0
and
\mathbb{E}[\eta_{i}^{2}|\mathcal{G}_{i}]\leq C_{\sigma}^{2}
.
We also have
\|\bm{x}_{i}\|\leq\sigma_{\min}^{-1},|\eta_{i}|\leq C_{0}\sigma_{i,h}^{-1}
and
\|\bm{x}_{i}\|_{\bm{Z}_{i-1}}=w_{i,h}
.
As a result,
|\eta_{i}|\min\left\{1,\|\bm{x}_{i}\|_{\bm{Z}_{i-1}}\right\}\leq C_{0}\frac{w_{i,h}}{\sigma_{i,h}}\leq\frac{C_{0}}{d^{2.5}H\mathcal{H}}
where the last inequality uses
\sigma_{i,h}^{2}\geq\mathcal{H}d^{2.5}H\|\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\|_{\bm{H}_{k-1,h}^{-1}}
.
By Lemma
I.3
, it follows that with probability
1-\frac{\delta}{H}
, for all
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\left[f(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}f](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}}=\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\bm{x}_{i}\eta_{i}\right\|_{\bm{Z}_{k}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq 8C_{\sigma}\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{K}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}d\lambda_{V}}\right)\log\frac{4HK^{2}}{\delta}}+\frac{4C_{0}}{d^{2.5}H\mathcal{H}}\log\frac{4HK^{2}}{\delta}.

Finally, on the event
\bigcap_{k\in[K]}\mathcal{A}_{k,h}
, we will have all the indicator functions equal to one.

(ii)

If
f(\cdot)
is a random function, covering arguments are used to handle the possible correlation between
f(\cdot)
and history data.

Denote the
\epsilon_{0}
-net of
\mathcal{V}
by
\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V},\epsilon_{0})
where
\epsilon_{0}=\min\left\{C_{\sigma}\sigma_{\min},\frac{\lambda_{V}C_{0}\sqrt{d}}{K}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\right\}
.
Hence, for any
f\in\mathcal{V}
, there exists
\bar{f}\in\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V},\epsilon_{0})
such that
\|\bar{f}-f\|_{\infty}=\sup_{s\in\mathcal{S}}|f(s)-\bar{f}(s)|\leq\epsilon_{0}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\left[f(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}f](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\left[\bar{f}(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}\bar{f}](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}}}_{\text{(i)}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\underbrace{\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\left[f(s_{i,h+1})-\bar{f}(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}(f-\bar{f})](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}}}_{\text{(ii)}}.

For term (ii), due to
\|\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\|\leq 1
and
|f(s_{i,h+1})-\bar{f}(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}(f-\bar{f})](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})|\leq\epsilon_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\left[f(s_{i,h+1})-\bar{f}(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}(f-\bar{f})](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\frac{K\epsilon_{0}}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}\sqrt{\lambda_{V}}}\leq\sqrt{d\lambda_{V}}C_{0}.

We next bound term (i) as follows.
On the event
\mathcal{A}_{k,h}
, by definition of
\epsilon_{0}
,

\displaystyle[\mathbb{V}_{h}\bar{f}](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\leq 2[\mathbb{V}_{h}f](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})+2[\mathbb{V}_{h}(\bar{f}-f)](s_{k,h},a_{k,h})

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{\sigma}^{2}\sigma_{k,h}^{2}+2\epsilon_{0}^{2}\leq 4C_{\sigma}^{2}\sigma_{k,h}^{2}.

For any fixed
f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{V}
, we set
\mathcal{G}_{i}=\mathcal{F}_{i,h}
,
\bm{x}_{i}=\sigma_{i,h}^{-1}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}
,
\eta_{i}=\sigma_{i,h}^{-1}\left[\bar{f}(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}\bar{f}](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]\ind_{\mathcal{A}_{k,h}}
and
\bm{Z}_{k}=\lambda_{V}\bm{I}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}^{\top}=\bm{H}_{k,h}
.
Moreover, due to the choice of
\sigma_{i,h}
, it follows that
|\eta_{i}|\min\left\{1,\|\bm{x}_{i}\|_{\bm{Z}_{i-1}}\right\}\leq C_{0}\frac{w_{i,h}}{\sigma_{i,h}}\leq\frac{C_{0}}{d^{2.5}H\mathcal{H}}
.
By Lemma
I.3
, it follows that with probability
1-\frac{\delta}{H}
, for all
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\sup_{f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V},\epsilon_{0})}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\left[\bar{f}(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}\bar{f}](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]\ind_{\mathcal{A}_{k,h}}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle 32C_{\sigma}\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{K}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}d\lambda_{V}}\right)\log\frac{4HK^{2}N_{0}}{\delta}}+\frac{4C_{0}}{d^{2.5}H\mathcal{H}}\log\frac{4HK^{2}N_{0}}{\delta}.

where
N_{0}=|\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V},\epsilon_{0})|
.

As a result, on the event
\bigcap_{k\in[K]}\mathcal{A}_{k,h}
, all the indicator functions equal to one, which completes the proof.

(iii)

The proof is almost similar to the second item except that we use Lemma
I.4
to analyze the term
(I)
.
Noticing we also have
|\eta_{i}|=|\sigma_{i,h}^{-1}\left[\bar{f}(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}\bar{f}](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]|\leq\frac{C_{0}}{\sigma_{\min}}
.
By Lemma
I.4
, it follows that with probability
1-\frac{\delta}{H}
, for all
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\sup_{f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V},\epsilon_{0})}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\sigma_{i,h}^{-2}\bm{\phi}_{i,h}\left[\bar{f}(s_{i,h+1})-[\mathbb{P}_{h}\bar{f}](s_{i,h},a_{i,h})\right]\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{k,h}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle\frac{C_{0}}{\sigma_{\min}}\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{K}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}d\lambda_{V}}\right)+\log\frac{N_{1}}{\delta}},

where
N_{1}:=|\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V},\varepsilon_{1})|
.

∎

Lemma I.6

(Lemma 11 in
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
)
.

Let
\{\bm{x}_{t}\}_{t\in[T]}\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
and assume
\|\bm{x}_{t}\|\leq L
for all
t\in[T]
. Set
\bm{\Sigma}_{t}=\sum_{s=1}^{t}\bm{x}_{t}\bm{x}_{t}^{\top}+\lambda\bm{I}
. Then it follows that

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\min\left\{1,\|\bm{x}_{t}\|^{2}_{\bm{\Sigma}_{t-1}^{-1}}\right\}\leq 2d\log\left(1+\frac{TL^{2}}{d\lambda}\right).

Lemma I.7

(Lemma 12 in
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
)
.

Suppose
\bm{A},\bm{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
are two positive definite matrices satisfying that
\bm{A}\succeq\bm{B}
, then for any
\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\|\bm{x}\|_{\bm{B}^{-1}}\leq\|\bm{x}\|_{\bm{A}^{-1}}\sqrt{\frac{\det(\bm{A})}{\det(\bm{B})}}.

The following lemmas are concerning function class and covering number, which are also used in
He et al. 2023
.
Let
\mathcal{K}=\left\{k_{1},k_{2},\cdots\right\}
denote the set of episodes where the algorithm updates the value function in Algorithm
3
. For a fixed number of episodes
K
, we have
|\mathcal{K}|\leq K
trivially.
Lemma
I.8
shows
|\mathcal{K}|
is only logarithmically related to
K
due to the mechanism of rare-switching value function updates.

Lemma I.8
.

|\mathcal{K}|\leq dH\log_{2}\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda_{R}\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda_{V}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right).

Proof.

According to the updating policy, for each episode
k_{i}
, there exists a stage
h^{\prime}\in[H]
such that
\det(\bm{H}_{k_{i}-1,h^{\prime}})\geq 2\det(\bm{H}_{k_{i-1}-1,h^{\prime}})
or
\det(\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{i}-1,h^{\prime}})\geq 2\det(\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{i-1}-1,h^{\prime}})
.
Since we always have
\bm{H}_{k_{i}-1,h}\succeq\bm{H}_{k_{i-1}-1,h}
and
\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{i}-1,h}\succeq\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{i-1}-1,h}
for all
h\in[H]
, it then follows that

\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\bm{H}_{k_{i}-1,h})\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{i}-1,h})\geq 2\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\bm{H}_{k_{i-1}-1,h})\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{i-1}-1,h}).

By induction, it follows that

\displaystyle\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\bm{H}_{h,k_{|\mathcal{K}|}-1})\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\bm{\Sigma}_{h,k_{|\mathcal{K}|}-1})\geq{}

\displaystyle 2^{|\mathcal{K}|}\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\bm{H}_{k_{1}-1,h})\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\bm{\Sigma}_{k_{1}-1,h})

\displaystyle\geq{}

\displaystyle 2^{|\mathcal{K}|}\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\lambda_{R}\bm{I})\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\lambda_{V}\bm{I})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle 2^{|\mathcal{K}|}(\lambda_{R}\lambda_{V})^{dH}.

On the other hand, due to
\bm{H}_{h,k_{|\mathcal{K}|}-1}\preceq\bm{H}_{h,K}
, the determinant
\det(\bm{H}_{h,k_{|\mathcal{K}|}-1})
is upper bounded by

\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\bm{H}_{h,k_{|\mathcal{K}|}-1})\leq\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\bm{H}_{h,K})\leq\left(\lambda_{R}+\frac{K}{\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}}\right)^{dH}.

A similar result holds for
\prod_{h\in[H]}\det(\bm{\Sigma}_{h,k_{|\mathcal{K}|}-1})
.
Finally, we have

|\mathcal{K}|\leq dH\log_{2}\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda_{R}\nu_{\mathrm{min}}^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda_{V}\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right).

The proof is completed.
∎

The optimistic value function
V_{h}^{k}(\cdot)=\min_{k_{i}\leq k}\max_{a}Q_{h}^{k_{i}}(\cdot,a)
belongs to the function class
\mathcal{V}^{+}

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}^{+}=\bigg\{f|f(\cdot)

\displaystyle=\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\min_{i\leq|\mathcal{K}|}\min\left\{\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,a),\bm{\theta}_{i}+\bm{w}_{i}\rangle+\beta_{R}\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,a)\|_{\bm{H}_{i}^{-1}}+\beta_{V}\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,a)\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{i}^{-1}},\mathcal{H}\right\},

(I.2)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\theta}_{i}\|\leq B,\|\bm{w}_{i}\|\leq L,\bm{H}_{i}\succeq\lambda_{R}\bm{I},\bm{\Sigma}_{i}\succeq\lambda_{V}\bm{I}\bigg\}.

while the pessimistic value function
\check{V}_{h}^{k}(\cdot)=\max_{k_{i}\leq k}\max_{a}\check{Q}_{h}^{k_{i}}(\cdot,a)
belong to the function class
\mathcal{V}^{-}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}^{-}=\bigg\{f|f(\cdot)

\displaystyle=\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\max_{i\leq|\mathcal{K}|}\max\left\{\langle\bm{\phi}(\cdot,a),\bm{\theta}_{i}+\bm{w}_{i}\rangle-\beta_{R}\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,a)\|_{\bm{H}_{i}^{-1}}-\beta_{V}\|\bm{\phi}(\cdot,a)\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{i}^{-1}},0\right\},

(I.3)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\theta}_{i}\|\leq B,\|\bm{w}_{i}\|\leq L,\bm{H}_{i}\succeq\lambda_{R}\bm{I},\bm{\Sigma}_{i}\succeq\lambda_{V}\bm{I}\bigg\}.

Here
B
and
L=\mathcal{H}\sqrt{\frac{dK}{\lambda_{V}}}
are uniform upper bounds of
\bm{\theta}_{i}
and
\bm{w}_{i}
respectively (See Lemma E.2 of
He et al. 2023
for its proof).

Lemma
I.9
gives the covering number of function class
\mathcal{V}^{+},\mathcal{V}^{-}
and the squared version.

Lemma I.9

(Covering number of value functions)
.

Let
\mathcal{V}^{\pm}
denote the class of optimistic or pessimistic value functions with definition in (
I.2
) and (
I.3
) respectively. And denote the squared version of
\mathcal{V}
as
\left[\mathcal{V}\right]^{2}:=\left\{f^{2}|f\in\mathcal{V}\right\}
.
Let
\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V},\epsilon)
be the
\epsilon
-covering number of
\mathcal{V}
with respective to the distance
\mathrm{dist}(f,f^{\prime}):=\sup_{s\in\mathcal{S}}|f(s)-f^{\prime}(s)|
.
Then,

\log\mathcal{N}(\mathcal{V}^{\pm},\epsilon)\leq\left[d\log\left(1+\frac{4(B+L)}{\epsilon}\right)+d^{2}\log\left(1+\frac{8d^{1/2}\beta_{R,K}^{2}}{\lambda_{R}\epsilon^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{8d^{1/2}\beta_{V}^{2}}{\lambda_{V}\epsilon^{2}}\right)\right]|\mathcal{K}|,

\displaystyle\log\mathcal{N}([\mathcal{V}^{+}]^{2},\epsilon)\leq\left[d\log\left(1+\frac{8H(B+L)}{\epsilon}\right)+d^{2}\log\left(1+\frac{32d^{1/2}H^{2}\beta_{R,K}^{2}}{\lambda_{R}\epsilon^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{32d^{1/2}H^{2}\beta_{V}^{2}}{\lambda_{V}\epsilon^{2}}\right)\right]|\mathcal{K}|,

where
|\mathcal{K}|
is the number of episodes where the algorithm updates the value function in Algorithm
3
.

Proof.

The proof is nearly identical to Lemma E.6, E.7, E.8 in
He et al. 2023
except for the following differences.
First, the weight in dot product is
\bm{\theta}_{i}+\bm{w}_{i}
instead of
\bm{w}_{i}
, so
L
is replaced by
B+L
in the results. Second, since we maintain two different matrices
\bm{H}_{i}
and
\bm{\Sigma}_{i}
, the term with respect to the extra matrix is added accordingly.
∎
